KISHI


~_A Tsakanin Zubda Jini🩸_~

 Mai_Dambu

*Sadaukarwa ga  Halimatu Kangiwa (halilsoss). Mrs J Moon. A'isha Fareedah Ya'u Nusaiba Sulaiman Ayuba jikalle na*
 *01*

*Rauta State*
20/May/2000
_remain 50days to Election_
Huci yake kamar zai cire zuciyar shi yana kallon fararra takardan da aka ajiye Mishi. Idanun shi yana tara kwalla. Murmushi yayi tare da furza da iskar bakin shi.

        Yana gyara zaman medical glass din shi, dattijon siyasa dan kimanin shekaru saba'in da bakwai. Dr Abdul Hamid Jamil Bagauda. Farin bafulatani kyakkyawar dashi dogo fari.

"Dr Yanzun abinda Excellency Rabi'u yayi mana" inji PA,
   Murmushi Bagauda yayi tare da lumshe idanun shi yayi kafin ya bude akan P.A,
"Ka kira Qarib ku tattara min kayana baki daya, ku bar musu office din su."

        "Toh Yallabai. Amma yan jam'iyyar suna waje." Inji P.A,

Gyada kai yayi tare da mik'ewa ya kwashi wayar shi da sauran kayan shi ya fito.
"Ba zamu tab'a lamunta ba, Dr Bagauda kai ne shugaba kuma jigon siyasan jahar Rauta, idan muka rasa ka ba zamu kuma."

"Wali meye muke yi da ba zamu bayyana sabon kungiyar mu ba, sai mun nemi taimakon wasu! Daga yau Dr Bagauda shine shugaban Jam'iyyar  Gaskiya Democracy Party."

       "Eyeeeee!" Suka fada da karfi suka kuma cewa.

       Hayaniyar da tayi yawa ce yasa P.A.
"Ku dakata! Mai Girma Shugaban Jami'ayyar Gaskiya Democracy Party zai magana."

   Fitowa yayi yana kallon su daya bayan daya, sanye suke da fararren kayan wacce take alamar kayan kungiyar ce suka ware ta personal. Jim yayi sannan ya kalle su bayan ya zare medical glass din shi.
"Ina jin tsoron tsayar da wanin ku! Ina jin tsoron sake baku damar tsayawa akan matsayin siyasa, Idan Hamidu Dan Ladi Barau ya fita bayan samun damar zama Senata, Rabi'u Gwarzo ya dakatar dani a shugaban jam'iyyar PLP ban ga laifin su ba,  Har yau Jahar Rauta tana niman hanyar magance wasu matsalolin da ya addabe su. .har yau mun kasa siyasa da akida kullum siyasa domin aljuhun mu muke.

    Ni nan dan kishin kasa ne Ni nan ina son kawo cigaba da canji a cikin jahata me yasa duk wanda aka zab'a yake juya min baya? Ban san me yasa suke boye kudirin su ba amma daga yau na ajiye siyasa har Abada."

   Yana fadan haka ya share kwallar idanun shi, bayan ya saka kai zai fita daga ofishin jami'ayyar.
"Wallahi bamu yarda ba, Bama yi Bama yi dole a dawo mana da shugaban mu."

Haka suka fita bakin hanya suna bin motar shi suna ihu. Bama yi Bama yi.

        Haka ya bar gurin tare da nufar hanyar gidan shi yana me jin b'acin rai a ranshi.

    ----
"Sallau yau naga Bankad'a da tijara wata watsatsiyar ce ta watsa min ruwa a barandan gidana? Bana ciki da Iskanci da rashin mutunci har wani ya isa ya kawo min Iskanci da watsewa, wannan sai na sha damara na buga gwatso musha dambe kowacce watsatsiyar"

       Duk babu wanda ya fito waje zunzurutun tsoron da Allah ya daura musu na Hajiya Turai, masifaffiyar mata ce gata ita daya ta mallaki katon gidan da ake haya dashi daga ita sai yaranta uku biyu Hassan da Hussaini, sai karamin su sallau.

Hassan bai da kwaranniya, amma kuma yana harkan kasuwancin shi da matar da ya auro a chadi dake yana bin babbar mota har can, me suna Zehra, fara kyakyawa shuwa Arab na Chad.

    Yayinda Husaini ya kasance yana bibiyar al'amuran siyasa, babban mabiyan Dr Bagauda ne, kuma yana samun Alkhairi a tare dashi.

       Sai Karime itace autarta, tana masifar kaunarta kamar tayi yayya, haka ma yan biyun ta, musamman Zehra da Allah ya daurata akan Hajiya Turai take masifar kaunar ta. Gata ba wata babba bace amma tasan yadda take tafiyar da uwar mijinta.

      A kaf unguwar kuka babu wanda bai san gidan Hajiya Turai Yar Jidali ba, domin ita Allah yayi mata mugun masifa da fada, zata iya wuni guda tana Bala'i da masifa bata gaji ba, haka kuma sai Allah ya daurawa Hassan hakuri da kawaici, bai iya fada ba. Saba'in Husaini da Allah ya daura mishi fada da niman tadda zaune tsaye.

           Amma kuma duk bala'in Hajiya Turai tana da mutunci da sanin ya kamata, kuma a fadin unguwar kaf bata ragawa kowa. Kuma masu haya a gidanta basu da ikon mata barna ba zaka biya ba amma kuma wallahi sai kayi dan sani, kuma tsafta wajibi ne, gyaran gida da shara wajibi ne, kuma ba a fada a gidanta, ba a gulma a gidan ta, ba a rigima.karewa dai Hajiya Turai Bala'i bata bawa Gwauro ko  gwauruwa hayar daki sai masu aure dan tace gidanta ba loja bane da za ayi ta kwaso mata ana  watsa mata zunubi akanta.

     Kuma wani hikimar da tayi bata tashi hada yaranta da mutnen gidan hayar ba, bude musu kofar su tayi ta bayan gidan, babu abinda ya dame ta. Kullum tana da wata dabi'a asuba nayi zata fito waje ta shimfid'a taburma da butar ta, da abin sallah ta, har sai kowa ya fito ya same ta tana sallah. Wannan yawan Ibadar nata ya sake kara mata kwarjini a idanun jama'ar gidan, har suma sun fahimci fada a jininta yake.

    "Hajiya don Allah kiyi hakuri, Nazirah ce ta watsa ruwa bata sani ba, kinsan halin dan yau ka haife shi baka haife halin shi ba"

     Shiru tayi ya kuma kaskantar da kanshi domin Hajiya Turai akwai bala'in son girma.
"Ayya Hajiya kiyi hakuri mana" inji Zehra da ta fito da abin karyawa.
"Sannun Yar nan, kina fama da tsofa gashi kina hidima dani, don Allah ki hakura haka mana"

    "Kai Hajiya babu wani hutu a tsakanin mu, dan haka gashi ki karya ayi hakuri ba zata kuma ba" inji Zehra,
Murmushi Hajiya Turai tayi sannan tace mata.
"Yar nan ai sunci albarkacin ki, maza tashi sallau ayi ta musu fada dan bana son rashin hankali. Allah ya bada sa'a."

         Shigowar Husain da bugun shinkafa, yasa Hajiya ta tsare shi da ido.
"Daga gidan Uban waye ka tawo da shinkafa? Hussain tun bayan mutuwar aurenka da Balaraba ka zama soko baki daya, yanzun baka jin kunyar Umar yasan abinda kake aikatawa meye a cikin bangar siyasa? Don Allah ka daina wannan abin wallahi ina tsoron kar wani abu ya same ku ba zan iya dauka ba na rantse da Allah."

           "Toh Hajiya amma dai ai wannan lamari ba na kowa na dauko ba, shugaban Jam'iyyar Peace Land Party ya bani domin sun kirani" yana magana irinta yan saba, takaici yasa ta katse shi da cewa.
"Kai ungo naka? Sakarai daga alamun abu ne suke shi yasa suka maka wannan shirin kar na kuma ganinka a cikin al'amarin siyasa wallahi sai na sab'a maka"

     "Toh Ni kome nayi sai nayi laifi wannan ma na kawowa matar Hassan ne tana mana girki tunda baku so shikenan ai zan je na sayar na samu na kaiwa wancan Yaron kudin da zasu saya mishi abinci."

Bakin ciki ya kama Hajiya Turai sosai dan haka cikin kallon takaici tace mishi.
"Allah ya wadaran naka ya lalace"
"Hajiya Addu'a zaki na musu Banda baki" gyada kai tayi tana kallon Zehra. Hassan da Hussaini Allah yayi musu mugun kama, kamar su tayi yawa, kuma abin ba a cika gane su ba. Sai dai ta hanyar Amfani da muryan su, idan Hassan yana magana a sanyayye ne, yayinda idan Hussain yana magana irinta yan iska ne.

        ***
Manyan yan jam'iyyar Gaskiya Democracy Party ne suka nufi gidan Bagauda kamar yadda suke tsammani haka ya amshe su hannu bibbiyu, yana me zama cikin mutumtaka.

Bayan an gaishe gaishe sai da suka kallo juna kafin suka ce.
"Mun sami hukumar zab'e munyi musu magana, tare da shigar da dan takarar da muke son ganin ya tsaya, Salahudeen Kabiru. Kayi wani abu saura kwanaki arba'in a tsayar da Campaign."

    "A'a ba wannan bane masalaha, muna niman Dr Bagauda ya dawo,ba dan mun isa ba. Ya duba kafaffun mu a kasa yan jam'iyyar mu suna bukatar shi."

Sunkuyar da kai yayi tare da kasa cewa kome.
"Don Allah kayi hakuri kamar yadda muka sanka a me kishin kasa ka nuna mana haka."
"Is too late na fito nayi wani abu a akan siyasa. Kuyi hakuri don Allah"

   Haka yaki accepter din su, daga nan manyan malamai da mutanen masana ilimin siyasa suka fara fashin bakin akan  rabuwa da Dr Bagauda, haka yayi matukar gigita hankalin Yan jam'iyyar Gaskiya Democracy Party.

Yayinda gefe guda Yan jam'iyyar Peace Land Party suke ta samun haɓakar kungiyar su.

  Sai da ya rage saura kwanaki ƙalilan za a rufe campaign, sannan Dr Bagauda ya amshi damar nuna Salahudeen Kabiru, a matsayin dan takarar Senata a garin Rauta, dan dama basu kai da sun tsayar da maganar Gwamna ba, iya kacin su Senata ne.

       Ana jibi zab'e hukumar zaben Najeriya ta saka doka tare da yarjejeniya a tsakanin Kungiyoyin yan takara. Dokar itace matukar aka kama dan kungiyar ku da sace a kwatin zab'e zaku shekara goma sha shida baku tsaya a kowacce matsayi ba, sannan kuma duk wanda aka kama da sace akwatin zabe a harbe shi. Kowaye ne,

        Gyada kai suka yi bayan sun amince da sharuddan da aka kafa musu. Haka suka mike tare da kallon juna suka yi sallama.

    .....
Ranar zab'e.

   Kwance take tana kallon shi yana ta shiryawa, jikinta a mace.
"Ni kam ba zaka hakura da zuwa zab'en nan ba kuwa? Wallahi bana jin dadin baki ɗaya."

        Zama yayi tare da riko hannun ta, yana murmushi.
"Insha Allah zan dawo lafiya lau gare ki, kadda kuri'a kawai zanyi na dawo ai kodan abin cikin ki zan dawo da wuri."

  "Don Allah ka kula"

         Haka ya mike har ya fita ya dawo yana niman wani abu sai da ya gama zaga dakin sannan ya dawo ya zauna a gaban ta, tare da sauke ajiyar zuciya.
"Ashe gaki nan. Ke nake ta nima ban ganki ba."

Gyada kai nayi tare da tashi zaune ina kallon shi.
"Gani nan"
Murmushi yayi sannan ya sumbaci goshinta, ya fita.

     ***
"Ka tabbatar abinda ka gaya min haka ne? Toh indai haka ne idan kome ya faru afka mishi,  domin dama haka nake so baki daya na durkusar da Bagauda, na hana shi walwala kamar kowa. Ya shekara goma sha shida bai d'aga hannun dan takara ba. Idan aka yi haka an gama min kome"

  "Haka ne yallabai ai ta karewa Dr Bagauda, yana ji yana gani shi da daura wani mai mulki sai a lahira"

Suka kwashe da dariya, sannan yace mishi.
"Ina son duk abinda za ayi Gaskiya Democracy Party su rasa  damar su, ina son na ga gazawar su, yadda zasu dawo Jam'iyar Peace Land Party. Wanna shine kudirina."

Dan haka suna gama abinda zasu tattauna aka kira yan daba da yan barandan siyasa, aka basu kayan maye sai da suka sha tatil sannan aka mika musu makamai.

     "Ka tabbatar sun saka kayan Jami'ayyar Gaskiya Democracy Party, sannan su sace akwatinan zab'en."

    "Toh Yallabai" haka ya basu kayan  GDP suka saka sannan suka nufi cikin gari tare da tadda tarzoma, bayan an hargitsa sassan gurin zab'en. A take aka bada Oda duk inda aka kama wanda ya dace akwatin zabe a kashe shi."

            Aikuwa kafin kace kwabo sojoji da yan sanda sun fara sakewa yan iskan nan wuta. Hussain da yake gudun ceton rayuwar shi, ya hadu da hassan daidai an sakewa Husain harbi, haka Hassan ya tsaya ya goye shi.

Suka kuma fantsamawa da gudu, yan sanda binsu suke,  harbin Hassan aka yi a kafa, garin gudu  ceton dan uwanshi. Ya jifa Hussain a kwata me dauke da ruwan kwata. Shi kuma ya zube a gurin, suna zuwa suka bude mishi wuta, sai da suka ga baya numfashin. Sannan suka fantsama niman Hussain.

    Wanda shima fadawar shi kwatar wani ƙarfe ya cake shi har sai da ya bula cikin shi zuwa bayan shi. Dakyar ya samu yafito, tare da ɗaukar gawan dan uwan shi yana tafiya yana hutawa.

      Garin ya hargitse sosai babu masaka tsinke, ko ina aka ratsa gawarwakin yan daba ne da basu ji ba basu gani ba an kashe su..

_A gaskiya wannan labarin a hargitse yake kuma yana dauke da wasu part part na kowa Ni mutum so mutanen da suke ganin ina sauka akan Jigon labarin na please And Please babu ruwanku da labarina a bar min kayana mana! Dole ne? Kaje ka karanta masu kyakyawan jigo a bar min kayana don Allah ina son abuna haka_

🖤KISHI..!!🖤�?
       ~_A Tsakanin Zubda Jini🩸_~

       Mai_Dambu

*Sadaukarwa ga Halimatu kangiwa (haliloss). Mrs J Moon. A'isha Fareedah Ya'u Nusaiba Sulaiman Ayuba jikalle na*
                  *02*

Faduwar gaba da Hajiya take ji babu iyaka, musamman idan ta kalli halin da Zehra take ciki sai jikinta yayi mugun sanyi, ta kidime ta mike gabanta faduwa, sai leka waje take ta dawo cikin gidan.

  Zehra kuwa tunda Su Malam Fatihu suka shigo gidan a guje gabanta ya fadi, ruwa ya fara zuba mata bata kuma fitowa ba, asalima tsoro ne ya cika mata zuciyarta.
       Fita Hajiya Turai tayi tana ratsa cikin gari, tana niman Yaranta. Hangota sojojin suka yi tare da daka mata tsawa.
"Ke tsohuwa baki da hankali ne?"

Murmushin da yafi kuka ciwo tayi sannan tace musu.
"Bani dashi! Idan naga yarana zanyi hankali." Bude mata ido suka yi Hajiya Turai ta nuna musu ita mahaukaciya ce, dole suka sararra mata, ta shiga niman yaran ta.
Allah da ikon shi kuwa suka yi kicibis da wasu yan taimakon agaji. Daya tasan shi munkaila.

      Yana ganinta ya sunkuyar da kanshi kasa.
"Baka gasu Yan biyu na ba?"
Kasa magana yayi yana me sunkuyar da kanshi kasa. Kallon shi tayi na wani lokaci taga yadda yake share kwalla, murmushi ta sake mishi. Allah ya bata wani irin tawwakali da hakuri. Dan haka ta leka bayan motar, ta hango su kwance jikin juna.

"Shine ba zaka gaya min ba? Ai ko babu kome ai Ni musulma ce, hakuri ya zame min dole, Allah ya jikansu da rahama."
Ta faɗa tare da shiga bayan motar, suka nufi gidan ta. A kofar gidan aka tsaya tare da fidda gawarwakin su. Aka kai cikin gidan.

      Tunda aka saka su a tsakar gidan Zehra ta fito ta ga gawar mijinta, a gurin ta fara gurnanin Haihuwa, da sauri aka kai ta d'akinta.

   Ko minti goma ba ayi ba Allah ya sauke ta lafiya ta samu Yarta mace. ana shiryin kai gawarwakin Hassan da Hussaini.

    Ana gamawa aka kirata, kasa magana tayi tana kallon fuskar mijinta, murmushi tayi tare da mik'ewa. Haka ta juya zuwa d'akin ta. 

       Abin tausayi haka aka kai su jana'izar su, sannan aka kai  su gidan su na gaskiya, sannan aka dawo zaman makoki.  Babu wanda yazo daga shashin yan siyasan nan, har aka gama zaman makokin Hajiya Turai ta tura da sakon rasuwar gurin dangin Zehra. 

Sun zo sun kwana biyu sannan suka tafi da ita bayan sun yiwa Hajiya Alkawarin kawo mata jikarta Aishafareeda. Wacce uwar tayi mata huduba.

---
Daga ɓangaren gwamna me ci wato Dr Rabi'u Adam Gwarzo babu wani alhini da jajjantawa sai ma nunawa da yayi akan b'ata gari ne suka farka hukumar zaben kuma anyi nasaran koransu amma suka ki tare da harbin yan sanda, bayan sun hadu da wasu yan bindiga suka kashe su.

     Dr Bagauda ya fito yayi alhini da jajjantawa iyalan waɗanda aka a kashe. Tare da tura musu da taimako bayan anyi bincike aka gano yan jam'iyyar su Ce yana ji yana gani aka dakatar da Jam'iyyar Gaskiya Democracy Party na tsawon shekaru goma sha shida. Bayan an rantsar da Gwamna Rabiu Gwarzo. Daga shi har yan jam'iyyar su, aka kafa sabuwar gwamnati me dauke da mugaye da azzaluman mutane, daga kowacce ƙabilar jahar Rauta.
  ***
2003/July/ 4th

Ahmad Bala Raees

A hankali ya shigo yana kananun mita, har ya zauna yana kallon Umma. Da take ta fama da wanke geron kamun da take sayarwa.

"Sannun Umma na"
"Yawwa Raees!"
Shiru yayi yana sosa kai tare da cewa.
"Ummana, dama maganar kudin ne."

     Gyada kai tayi sannan tace mishi.
"D'aga kasar matashin nan akwai kudin ka dauka iya wanda zai isheka."

  Gyada mata mai yayi yana murmushi. Sannan ya dauki kudin yayi mata sallama. Har ya isa bakin kofar fita daga gidan tace mishi.
"Idan kaje ka gaishe da Halima, dan nasan itace zaka je gurin ta."

     Cikin jin kunyar Umman shi ya dawo da baya yana sosa kanshi.
"Umma taya kika sani?"
"Hmm,.jiya kake bawa su Hadiza labarin budurwan ka"
"Umma na zata kina barci ne?"
"Idanuna biyu."

Ta faɗa mishi tana Cigaba da aikinta.
"Sai na dawo" "Allah yasa"
Ahmad Bala Raees,

Su din yan asalin garin tira ne dake karamar hukumar takansh dake garin, Fulanin Tira ne su. Ahmad yana da kane hudu mata biyu da namiji daya.

Azeema ita take bin bayan shi, sai Salim wanda yake bin Azeema sai Khadijah yar autar su.

Idan ana niman Jarumar Uwa aka samu Umma an gama, domin babu sana'ar da bata yi ba sabida Ahmad yayi karatun shi. Ta sayar da kosai ta sayar da awara babu sana'ar da bata yi ba har da kasuwan ƙauyuka ta shiga.

Umma Ramlat Allah yayi mata sanyin hali da na kyauta, tana da Alkhairi sosai. Kuma har yau baki daya unguwar su babu wanda bai san ta ba. Mutanen unguwar kuka suna matukar mutuntatta.

     Yanzun Ahmad ya gama karatun shi na Psychology.  matsayin master, yana ta faffutikar niman aiki ne amma yanayin kasar mu sai a hankali, duk wannan fadi tashin da yake Umma ce ta tsaya mishi domin Malam Adamu ya rasu ya barta da yara huɗu. Dangin Uban su kuwa ba wani karfi ne dasu ba, Umma ta saka rai akan Yaronta shi yasa tayi ta niman kudi ya tafi har jami'a, da sana'ar kamu da koko.  kuma tana kosai. Khadijah tana sayar da kayan koli, salim shima yana zama a kofar gidan da yar karamar shagon shi yana sayar da bakin mai dama sana'ar Ahmad din ne, yanzun ma da ya gama jami'ar abinda yake faffutikar yi kenan.

    Nuhu abokin shi ne tun yaranta, abokin shi da suka yi gwagwarmayyar rayuwa, amma shi yana aiki da babban Asibitin rauta. Shi yake auren Azeema, har da yaran su biyu.

       Da wannan sana'ar Allah ya taimaka ta aurar da Azeema, yanzun dashi yake biyan kudin Makarantar Salim, kasancewa yana Poly.

     ----
Da vespa din shi ya isa kofar gidan su Ishrat Sadeeq Kakari. Su din yan garin Kakari ne, Alhaji Abubakar Sadeeq Kakari attajiri ne kuma dan kasuwa, yana da Yara Maza biyu sune manyan sai Yan mata huɗu.

Yayun Ishrat, wacce asalin sunanta Halimatu. Tana da Yayarta da take auren wani dan Majilisar dattawan, me suna Raliya, sai Hibbatu wacce take auren Sojar ruwa tana aure a Lagos. Sai Hishmah tana auren wani Commissioner na lafiya tana nan Rauta. Sai dai ita tunda Allah ya had'ata da Ra'is ta haukace.

    Ga manyan mutane da attajirai da yan siyasa amma bata taɓa d'aga ido ta kalle su ba, duk da mahaifin su bai da damuwa duk wanda Yar shi ta kawo an gama magana. Mai matsalar mahaifiyar su ce, sai dai dake Allah ya daura mata son Yar autar ta yasa bata iya lankwasa ta.

       Yana isa ya hango ta, ta juyawa wani Alhaji baya, dan ba son shi take ba, abokin mijin Raliya ne, shi kuma ya nace da sonta. Tana ganin Ahmad bata sallame mutumin ba, ta nufi gurin Ahmad.
"Kaga dauke ni mu bar unguwar nan" ta faɗa tana hawa bayan mashin dinshi. Babu musu ya dauke ta, suka bar unguwar.

    Basu yi nisa ba, ya tsaya a gefen wani gida.
"Yau shekaran mu hudu me yasa ba zaka shigo gidan mu ba, Abban mu bai da matsala, Hajiya kuwa abinda nake so zata bani. Don Allah Ra'is ka shigo gidan mu mana."

     Murmushi yayi sannan yace mata.
"Ba wai bana son ki bane, Ishrat bani da hanyar da zan aure ki ne, bani da dukiyar da zan kashe miki ne, Ishrat ke kin saba da ganin kome an ajiye miki idan kika aure ni kome sai kin tambaya bani da arzikin da zan iya baki don haka ina."

"Ba arziki nake nima ba, domin ina da masu arzikin kai nake so! Kuma yau insha Allah zan gabatar da sunanka, don Allah karka watsa min kasa a idanu."

   Gyada kai yayi domin baya jin zai iya kin Ishrat sai dai bai da halin da zai iya bajintar auren ba. Suka sha soyayyar su, sannan ya maida ita gidan su, inda suka haɗu da Yayanta Yusuf. Bai wani damu can da tsawalawa ba, sai Habibu ne ma yake da matsalar saka ido, bayan sun gaisa da Raees ya juya yayi tafiyar shi.

    Tunda Ishrat ta shiga gidan ake tashin hankali da ita, domin ta hautsuna gidan da tashin hankali, har sai da aka tafi kasuwa aka gayawa Alhaji Abubakar Dan Kamusho, yazo da kanshi, yana jin abinda take so, bai d'aga hankalin shi ba. Sai ma tambayar da yayi mata.

   "Kina son Ahmad? Kuma Meye sana'ar shi?" Cikin kuka tace mishi.
"Abba ya gama master din shi, sannan yana kasuwancin sayar da bakin mai, Abba wallahi talaka ne amma mai zuciya, don Allah Abba karka ce a'a"

       "Shikenan Halimatu ince ba zaki dawo kiyi kuka da shi ba, domin bai da abinda zai iya rike ki? Bai da motar da zai baki kyauta, talaka ne sosai. Halima ba zan miki auren dole ba, tunda nasan ke ba ma Kinga mutanen da suka yi ta niman soyayyarki. Dan haka ba zan kara ba ya turo nan da wata biyu kacal ayi bikin ku." 

    Haka ta amince dukda Hajiyar su bata so ba, amma ba zata iya hana yar autar su abinda take so na.
Da dare da Ahmad yazo ta gaya mishi hankalin shi ya tashi, bayan ya koma gida ya samu umma da maganar, shiru tayi bata ce kala ba, har ya tafi dakin shi. 

Washi gari da sassafe ta nufi sabuwar unguwar Joni, ta gayawa Baffan Ahmad, tare da gaya mishi tana da karsananta guda biyu, sai awakai wanda take kiwon su a gidanta, sai akwai shsshin da ta gyarawa Ahmad din ko zai yi aure. Baffan yaji dad'i, tare da yaba mata irin kokarin ta.

     Kwana biyu tsakani dangin mahaifin Ahmad suka nufi gidan su Ishrat kuma ikon Allah an bashi aurenta, aka yanke kome.

   Mahaifinta dattijon arziki, yace ya yafe kayan lefe, amma haka Umma ta hada kayan lefe na ban mamaki, ta bada aka kai gidan bayan an kai sadaki dubu ashirin lokacin kudi na da daraja.

     Wata biyun da aka yanke kafin lokacin Umma ta gyara shashin sa,.an fidda mishi kofa ta waje, sai daya ta cikin gidan ta ja musu pampo ta gyara gidan yadda duk wanda yaga gidan zai gane ba karamin kokari tayi ba.

     Haka kafin a fara bikin sai da addana kudin da zasu nima bayan bikin, ga kayan abincin da ta saya ta ajiye musu. Dama geron kunu da waken kosai buhu buhu take dauka.

    ..... Haka akayi bikin Cikin rufin asiri da kwanciyar hankali, aka kawo mata Surukarta har d'akinta, itama iyayen ta sun mata shatara na arziki.

     Bayan bikin kowa ya watse umma ta cigaba da kula da harkokin ta, da na Yaranta, gashi lokacin Ahmad yayi hidimar biki da kudin hannun shi na bakin mai, dole ita ta kuma cire kudi a jikinta aka sake zuba sabon jari.

    Umma tana da nata hidimar haka take ajiyewa tana yiwa Ahmad da matar shi, bata tab'a b'ata rai ko sau Daya, asalima kamar gani take babu kome dan tayiwa Danta hidima,

  Tsakanin Amarya da Ango love ake bugawa, musamman da ya fahimci shine ya fara ta'adin matar shi ya kara haukace mata, hidima sosai yake mata, idan Yan uwanta suka zo basu tab'a barin gidan sai ya nima musu kudin abin hawa. Haka ya kara musu kwarin gwiwar son Kanwar su ta zauna dashi yana da kirki da kyautattawa mutanen da suke zagaye dashi musamman dangin Ishrat.

Itama a duniyar ta bata da na Biyun mahaifiyar shi, kamar ta kwanta Umma ta bi kanta.. itama kuma Umma babu ruwan ta da harkan su, domin bata son reni, bata shiga harkan su. kullum da safe zata shigo ta gaida Umma, tayi hira sannan ta koma shashinta tayi musu girki tana sakawa Umman.

          Duk da suma suna da nasu kayan abincin, haka bai hana ta saka musu ba.

        Yan uwan shi basu da gurin hira sai part din su, kuma itama bata da matsala, gata da hankali da sanin Ya kamata.....

       ~_A Tsakanin Zubda Jini🩸_~

       Mai_Dambu

*Sadaukarwa ga Halimatu kangiwa (haliloss). Mrs J Moon. A'isha Fareedah Ya'u Nusaiba Sulaiman Ayuba jikalle na*
                  *03*

Aishafareeda.
2007-December-04

Yar matashiyar yarinya da bazata wuce  shekara takwas ne rike da kofar dakin da Mahaifiyar ta take ciki, jama'ar gidan suna kuka. 
"Aishafareeda" a ka kira sunanta suka riko hannunta, zuwa dakin Hajja Aliyah. Rungume ta cikin kika da ban tausayi.
"Allah Sarki, Sannun Fareeda kin zama mareniya babu uwa babu Uba. Allah ka jikan Fatimah."

     Duk da bani da hankali a cikin jikina amma ina tsoron kar dai Ummi na ce ta rasu, dan haka na d'ago kai na kalli hajja Aliyah.
"Mama meye ya samu Ummina?"
Ina kallon fuskarta,
"Allah ya amshi rayuwarta."
Kwanciya nayi a jikin ta, domin na tuna Ummi tana yawan gaya min cewa.
"Aishafareeda Abbinki kashe shi aka yi sabida hidimar siyasa, kuma Allah ba zai bar su ba, domin ban yafe musu ba, dake namiji ce Tabbas sai na saka kin zama mai daukar fansa, sai na saka kin bi hakkin Mahaifinki Amma babu kome Allah zai bi mana hakkin mu"

    Haka tayi ta gaya min abubuwan da ya faru, duk da ba daukar wasu nake ba amma ina matukar kaunar Mahaifiyata.

  Ana gama mata sutura suka fita da ita. Kukan gidan ne ya karu dan haka na fito ina kallon su, haka na koma dakin na kwanta a gadon da muke kwana da Ummina.

      Babu wanda ya lura ina dakin, sai daga baya suka zo rufe dakin aka ganni kwance a gadon, daukata Aunty Neena tayi tare da cewa.
"Muje kisha magani jikinki babu dad'i."
Domin zazzaɓi ne ya rufe ni sosai, tana kai ni dakin ta bani magana na sha, sannan na kwanta. Ban yi kuka ba amma kuma a cikin kwanaki nima kamar zan bi mamana, domin rashin lafiya nayi sosai kamar zan mutu, haka suka yi ta daura min ruwa da jini, kafin kwana uku kakata Hajiya Turai tazo daga Nigeria, ganin yadda na koma yasa ta rokon arzikin su bata ni, idan na ji sauki zata dawo dani.

    Babu yadda suka yi domin kuwa sun san dole wata rana zan koma gurin ta, tunda wa'adin da aka diba idan da nisan kwana shekara goma ne zan yi, dan haka suka amince, ta tawo dani bayan anyi addu'ar bakwai, akan zata dawo dani addu'ar arba'in.

   Tunda muka shiga garin  ya burge ni, na kuma ji a raina kamar na shigo cikin dangina ne, Hajiya da kanta ta ware min daki me kyau aka zuba min kayan kawa ne yara. Ko ina gwanin ban sha'awa.

      Tunda na shigo gidan kome na gidan ya sauya, idan gari ya waye babu wanda ya isa yayi kwaranniya da kaya, sai na tashi a barci, kuma babu surutu. Domin kwana na daya da zuwa na fito tsakar gidan na rike ƙatuwar teddy bear na ce musu.

"Hajja Kaka, ni bana son hayaniya idan ina barci, kuma bana son surutu duk wanda ba zai yi kome a hankali ba ya tafi ya bar gidan."
Na fada ina zaro kananun idanuna.
"Toh dai kun ji? Toh a kiyayye a zauna lafiya."  Ta faɗa tare da kallon su, sanin cewa gidan Hajiya yana daga cikin gidajen da ake kyautatawa dan haya duk da kasancewar ta mafadaciya yasa suka yi mubaya'a.

       Haka na cigaba da daura mulkina bana fita ko ina, sai ranar da Umar ya shigo da mugun gudu, yana haki.

      "Hajja Kaka waye wannan?" Na tambaye ta Umar,
"Lusarin dan uwanki ne, bai da zuciya yanzun zaki samu koro shi suka yi."

Mik'ewa nayi tare da cewa.
"Muje" na riko hannun shi muka fito waje, wasu yara ne da basu wuce shi ba.

"Kai meye yayi muku?"
"Laaa! Babangida kaga baturiyan cikin tv nan da muke gani"
" Kai dalla can, meye zaki yi idan muka gaya miki"

"Zanci Ubanku ne a sanyayye." Na fada ina kallon su.
   
Cikin jin haushi suka hada baki tare da cewa.
"Ke yarinya karama har kin iya zagin mutane yau sai mun zane ki"

"Dauko min wancan kananun duwatsun."

    A hankali ya dauko min nace musu.
"Zaku tafi ko sai na fasa muku kanku?"
"Dukar ki zamu yi kuma mu daki dan uwanki."

Wato da na dauki dutsen na cab'e cab'e sama, na saita shi akan su na shiga jifar su, da gudu suka bar kofar gidan mu, juyawa nayi tare da cewa.
"Ka zama banza wallahi" na shiga cikin gidan. Shi ya bawa Hajiya labarin abinda nayi musu, bana hayaniya bana magana sosai. Ina son a rayuwata na ga ana min fadanci dan haka ina zaune Umar ya shiga min fadanci.

          ----
Gidan su Umar muka je na gaida Mamar shi, a hanyar dawowa muka tsaya a wani shago zamu sayi alawa. Wata yarinya ce tazo ta bangaje ni, juyawa nayi na daki kirjinta.
"Ke baki da hankali ne?"
"Kutt yar karama dake kin iya gayawa mutum maganar banza"

Mikawa Umar kudin nayi ina cewa.
"Bari na koya miki hankali." Na cire takalmi na, na kwala mata a fuskar ta.

Yana zuwa bai bi ba'asi ba ya kore mu a shagon, ni kuwa nace a bani kudi na ko alawata.
"Ba zaki tafi ba sai na zane ki"
Dauke kai nayi tare da mikawa me shagon hannuna.
"Zo mu tafi mana babu lokaci fa"

        Amsar al'awar yayi ya watsa min. Na kalle shi da sauri na dauka nima na watsa mishi ina kallon shi. Abun mamaki bai tab'a ganin yarinya mara kunya irin wannan yarinyar ba. Sauka yayi a mashin din tare da karyo bulalar maina. Ina kallon shi.
"Raees karka janyo mana masifar Hajiya Turai, barta bakuwa ce ba yar gari bace"

         Kura mata ido yayi ya ga itama shi take kallo.
"Ba dan ina sauri ba sai na zane ki sai dai kakarki tayi abinda zata yi mara kunya kawai."

Murguda mishi baki nayi tare da dauke kaina ina kallon Umar na ja hannun shi muka bar gurin.

  "Nuhu kasan yarinyar ne haka?"  Ya tambaye abokin shi.
"Eh mana, mahaifinta yana kai kaya Chad a can ya auri mahaifiyarta wallahi mutumin kirki itama matar shi haka, kashe su aka yi akan bangan siyasa. Ka tuna Hassan da Hussaini nan? Wanda dayan yake yawo kullum da kayan Jami'ayyar GDP?"

"Eh na tuna shi wani dogo baki, yar shi ce?"
"A'a yar Hassan din shi ce, itama yarinyar bata jima da zuwa ba sai dai kuma mahaifiyarta  ta rasu ne tazo nan kwana biyu. Abinda yasa na gaya maka ma sabida Rabe abokin Jamil ne yake gaya musu, kyan yarinyar shi yasa da naganta da Umar na Fahimci haka, ai shi Umar din mahaifiyar shi tana aure a bayan layin mu ne."

     Shiru yayi suna tafiya har  suka tsaya inda zasu  zo kusa da gidan Hajiya Turai, domin duk unguwar kuka suke matsalar layi ne ya rabasu,  yana tsaye suna magana da wani mutum.

   Da uwar harara na bi bayan su, sannan na saka Umar a gaba ina masifa ina gaya mishi magana, har muka isa  na ganshi a kusa da gidan mu, dauke kai nayi.

Shima kuma yana ta uzurin shi haka ya d'ago dai dai zan shiga gidan.
*Ai Nan ne gidan Hajiya Turai?* Ya tambayi kan shi.

    Yana gama abinda ya kawo su, suka yi tafiyar su ya manta da ita baki daya a tarihin shi niman aikin shi kawai yasaka a gaba.

   ---- Ana jibi arba'in Ummina Hajja kaka ta maida ni Chad, sai da aka yi addu'a sannan ta dawo, ta barni da kewar ta. 
Alhaji Mustapha Nasir Mai turare . Shine cikakken sunan Kakana, sana'ar shi kenan turare wanda ya gada tun daga iyayen shi da kakanin shi, gidan su Ummina ana kiran shi gidan turare, ko ya ka shiga gidan sai ka fita da kamshin.

   Sannan Kakata da sauran yan gidan suna aikin gyaran amare,dilke hatta sabulun wanka na, idan zaka dauka sai ka rantse dilke ne. Na taso inda ake mutuntta kamshi ake daraja kamshi.

   Ummina itace ya ta kusan goma a cikin gidan, kuma wani abin mamaki rabo ya haɗa auren, domin idan ba haka ba babu abinda zai saka su bashi auren Ummina.

   Familyn su Ummi sun fi yin auren dangi sama da na bare, shi yasa da Abbana yazo aka yi ta mamaki, gidan zagaye yake da yan uwa  Hajja Aliyah kakar su Ummina Hajja Nusaiba kuma kakana wacce ta haifi su Ummina, gidan babban gida ne me dauke da mutane sama da talatin.

     Hajja Nusaiba tana da kishiyoyinta, biyu Hajja Mairo da Hajja Hawwa, kuma duk daukan nan suna da Yara, kakar du Ummi tana mugun son Ummina, kasancewar ita ta rike Ummi har aurenta. Mace ce mai tsafta da son kamshi. Shi yasa nima na taso da kamshi domin a gurin Ummina naji haka itama ma'abociyar son kamshi ce.

     Gidan akwai kananun Yara sama da hamsin domin Yara mazan gidan wato Yayun Ummina su ashirin da biyu ne, su Ummi su bakwai  ne mata, kuma itace ta biyu a mata, ban tab'a ganin mutanen da suke kaunar nasu sama da yan uwan Ummina ba, domin daga wanda suke ciki daya har zuwa wanda suke Uba daya duk suna kyautata min.

        Haka yasa na taso cikin wani irin gata na fitar hankali, na taso a sangarce bana iya kome sai wanda aka min, makarantar da da Yaran manyan suke shi aka samu, amma sai dai nan da shekara biyu zan koma garin mu, gurin Hajja Kaka shima wani abin alfahari ne.

     Amma a Chad bani da abokin fada bani da masu tsokana kowa ririta Ni yaƙe.

   ***

2008-January-16

Ofishin gwamna.
Kallon Shugaban Jam'iyyar PLP yayi tare da cewa.
"Kana nufin jam'iyyar mu tana fuskarta barazana?"

Zuba mishi ido yayi yana jin babu dadi kafin yace mishi.
"Yallabai haka ne, suna ta komawa jam'iyyar Gaskiya Democracy Party, sabida matukar wa'adin da aka diba musu ya cika sune da karfin girgizar garin nan  baki daya."

    "Shikenan" ya faɗa bayan ya mike akan kujeran shi yana zaga gurin zaman shi. Yana nazarin abinda yake shirin Faruwa, yayi butulci ga Dr Bagauda ba iya shi ba har dasu har dasu Sanata Barau.

       Tabbas Dr Bagauda babban jigon siyasa ne, sai dai yadda yake ne ba zai yiwu ya cigaba da abinda yake ba ne, domin akwai karfin iko akan al'amarin siyasar jahar ɗin.

***
Shiru suka yi suna jiran Abinda Dr Bagauda zai ce, gyara zaman medical glass din shi yayi sannan yace musu.
"Qarib Uban Yaya shine Shugaban Jam'iyyar GDP, kasancewar shi mutumin da yake fadi tashi dan tsayawar jam'iyyar shi yasa naga dacewar watakila ya iya taka rawa gani Sosai"

           "Yallabai wannan matsayin bai min nauyi ba kuwa? Gaskiya A duba maganar nan ga Alhaji Sani P.A shi ya dace da matsayin shugaban kungiyar tunda kai kaki amsar ka tsaya a matsayin Jigon Jam'iyyar GDP." Inji Qarib Uban Yaya,

    Cikin hanzari Alhaji Sani P.A ya ce.
"A'a ina wani kayan sai Amale babban goro sai magogin karfe. Ai  da icce mai kama ake kota, ko kasan da arzikin a garin wasu gwara a garin mu, kai ka dace da wannan matsayin bamu ba. Dan haka zamu gina Jam'iyyar GDP da zuciyar mu da jikin mu, kuma muyi hakuri Insha Allah zamu yi nasara, kar mu tab'a su. Domin kuwa haka zai iya tab'a mana kimar mu,Insha Allah tun daga kan muƙamin chairman har zuwa kan Governo'r sai mun isa gurin da izinin Ubangiji karku manta Gaskiya muka ambata dan haka Insha Allah zamu ci ribar shi."

         "Insha Allah munyi mubaya'a." Inji sauran membobin Jam'iyyar. Gyara murya Dr Bagauda yayi sannan yace musu.
"P.A Alhaji Sani mun baka sakataren Jam'iyar ta jaha, sai Maadam Ruth Mai Hamada mun baki Shugaban Mata na Jam'iyyar, sai Alhaji Wali mun bashi ma'ajin Jam'iyyar, Malam Tanimu Rata mun bashi Mai shige da fice, Kallamu na Ta'alla mun bashi Shugaban matasan Jam'iyyar. Sai Habila Bature Nakama mun bashi P.R.O na jam'iyyar Hajiya Rahila Me Tukunya Mai yad'a labarai na musamman.. Ina fatan zamu rike gaskiya da Amana domin kawo canji a Jam'iyyar GDP, ina mai rokon ku karku biyawa kowa ayi rigima da ku, ku kasance jakadai na kwarai."

     Daga haka suka mike tare da rantsuwar zasu zama jakadai na kwarai.

   Bayan an gama taron kenan aka fito kawai sai ga Yan Sara suka. Suka nufi ofishin jam'iyyar GDP.  Dole aka rufe kofar shiga ofishin. Yan saran sai jifa suke tare da barazanar sai sun kashe Dr Bagauda. Ganin kar a salwantar da rayukan wadanda basu ji ba basu gani ba ya fito daga cikin ofishin.

     Ai kuwa suka haura mishi da kai mishi sara, kamar zai kashe shi. Allah ya kawo yan sanda.

     Aka nufi asibiti dashi cikin gaggawa aka fara niman ceto rayuwar shi.
***
"Daga nan gidan Radio gama gari, dake mita casa'in da hudu da digo biyar. Ni Amirah Mai nasara zan kawo muku labaran rana,. a yau muka samu labarin gwamnatin kasar nan ta saka hannu a sabon kudirin dokar hana auren jinsi.  Sai labarin da muka samu Mai girma Shugaban kasa ya gabarta da Dr Rabi'u Gwarzo gwamna  a matsayin mai wakiltan gwamnonin Kasar nan zuwa taron Opel na duniya. Sai labarin da muka samu na an kaiwa Babban jigon siyasar harin nan harin Dr  Abdul Hamid Jamil Bagauda. Inda yake can rai kwai-kwai mutu kwai-kwai."

   "Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihi rajioun! Raees kaji ko sun mai da siyasar  abin tashin hankali meye Dr Bagauda yayi musu?"

Zuba bakin mai yake yana murmushin takaici. Kamar ba zai magana ba, yana jin su baki daya suna ta caccakar gwamnatin jahar Rauta. Kafin yace musu.

⚪🖤KISHI..!!🖤�?
       ~_A Tsakanin Zubda Jini🩸_~

       Mai_Dambu

*Sadaukarwa ga Halimatu kangiwa (haliloss). Mrs J Moon. A'isha Fareedah Ya'u Nusaiba Sulaiman Ayuba jikalle na*
                   *4*

"Kun san me yake faruwa?" Ya kafe su da idanun shi, bayan ya juya yana bada canji. 

"Kai banza idan kasan na zaka gaya mana ba meye na wani ja mana aji? Mutum sai kace mace" murmushi yayi sannan ya koma gurin zaman shi yace musu.
"Baku bani amsa ba, sai hayagagga"

"Toh! Raees me zamu ce maka? Bayan kana son sanar mana da abinda bamu sani ba" suka fada tare  da kafe shi da ido. Jan iska yayi sannan ya fesar bayan ya gama ja musu rai kamar ba zai magana ba yace.
"Ban da ra'ayi a harkan siyasa, amma zan so naga Dr Abdul Hamid Jamil Bagauda. Sabida mutumin da yake da kyakyawan manufar siyasa irin shi dole kaso ganin shi.

     Kuma dole ya fuskanci rayuwar da baraza, amma bayan haka bana tunanin zai kai labari, domin ba yau aka fara ba, kuma ba yau za a daina ba, dan haka ina mishi fatan sauki."

   Shiru suka yi kafin suka mishi caaa.
"Dan kutumar kaniyar, dan iska dama akan haka ka samu yin shiru dan banza mara mutunci." inji Sadeeq, dukkan su abokan shi ne. Murmushi yayi yana Cigaba lissafin da yaƙe.
"Ya Raees kazo an gama abinci inji Aunty Ishrat" inji Kanwar shi Khadijah, tana murmushi.
"Karbo min anan zan ci yau" ya faɗa mata, da sauri ta juya cikin gidan, ta gayawa Ishrat.

Sake baki tayi tana kallon Khadijah.
"Yanzun don Allah haka zai min? Yaushe aka yi bikin mu da zai fara cin abinci a waje? Gashi abincin iya mu nayi fa" ta faɗa kamar zata yi kuka.
"Laa! Aunty Ishrat indai ba zai ishe ku ba, bari na shiga cikin gidan gurin Umma na amshi nata, sai na kawo a saka mishi ko?"
"A'a bari na dafa taliya, dauka ki kai mishi." Da sauri jikin Khadijah na rawa ta dauka,tare da fita da abincin, shiga kitchen ɗin tayi tare da kunna risho ta kuma daura abincin.

    Sai cika take tana batsewa sabida kawai yace a kai mishi abincin shi waje.
"Aunty wai ki bani ruwa"
"Toh" ta faɗa a dakile, ita kam Khadijah bata san dalilin fusatar ta ba, amma ta maida hankali akan ko bata da lafiya ne..

       Mika mata katon jug tayi da ruwa, ta rufe ita kuma yarinyar ta saka kai zata fita tace mata.
"Idan kin dawo ki koma gurin Umma zan kwanta kaina na ciwo"
"Toh" tace cikin sigar tausayi, sannan ta wuce abinta.

Koda ta dawo bata bi gidan ba, ta kofar su ta wuce ta sami Umma tana aikinta, suka kama tare, suna yi suna hira.
"Umma Aunty Ishrat bata da lafiya"
"Shi yasa na ganki da wuri, tayi girki kuwa?"
"Eh tayi har na kaiwa Ya Raees nashi. Itace dai ta kwanta"
"Toh Allah ya sawwaka"
"Amin" dake Umma dan wake take, shi yasa ta nutsu suka gama, sannan ta zuba Na Raees da matar shi, su kuma suka juye sauran a babban abu, sannan ta watsa mishi ruwan zafi, domin Salim zai dawo daga makaranta.

    ***
A babban asibitin kuwa yan Jam'iyyar GDP ne cike, ana fatan Allah ya bawa Dr Bagauda lafiya, tare da nutsuwa suna jiran sakamakon.

     Lokacin da likitocin suka fito jikin su yayi sanyi kasancewar har da Yaran shi da matan shi.
"Dr wani hali Baban mu yake ciki?"
" Toh da farko zamu ce Alhamdulillahi, toh sai dai kafar shi da aka sare dole zamu cire, sabida kwayar cutar taitanus ya kama,kuma idan na a cire ba zai iya tab'a jikin shi baki ɗaya."

Wani irin kuka suka fashe dashi musamman da suka ji ance Babban yar Dr Abdul Hamid Jamil Bagauda ta saka hannu a cire kafar."

  Wani irin kuka take har ta saka hannun sannan, suka koma gefe. Ranar wuni suka yi a asibitin hankalin su a tashe, basu sami nutsuwa ba sai karfe goma na dare bayan an fito dashi, yana barcin wahala.

   Kuka da uwargidan shi take gwanin ban tausayi, tana faɗin ya hakura da siyasar tunda kashe shi ake son yi, ai babu amfanin haka, haka yan jam'iyar suma hankalin su a tashe yake.

  Washi gari
Aka tashi da wata irin zanga zanga, wanda ya haifar da mugun tashin hankali, da zubda jini. Garin ya kicime dole aka watsa sojoji da yan sanda, domin kwantar da tarzoma. Sannan mai girma Gwamna Alhaji Rabi'u Gwarzo yayi magana akan lallai basu da masaniya akan wannan ta'annatin da aka yi, sannan sun yi alawadarar abinda ya faru.

    Bayan nan sun kuma jajjantawa Iyalan Dr Abdul Hamid Jamil Bagauda, abisa harin da aka kawo mishi. Sannan mai girma Gwamna ya shirya kwamitin bincike akan wannan al'amarin.

     Bayan nan an sako wasu masana alamarun yau da kullum, na siyasar duniya, kuka.

  "Kai tsaye kuka sauraron gidan radiyo Gama gari FM tare da ni Rahama Ayman, a gefe guda muka tare da babban masanin al'amuran siyasar duniya,. Cikin shirin mun nan me suna zuwa da kai yafi sako! Barka da zuwa Dr Bukar Dalibi." 

"Barka dai Rahama Ayman"
"Toh kai tsaye Dr zamu tsunduma ka'in dana'in cikin shirin, Dr shin ta wata fuska kake kallon alamuran siyasar duniya da ta kasar mu har zuwa ga jahar mu? Shin wani sauyi ake samu akan siyasar kasar mu da ta jahar mu?"

"Alhamdulillahi. Nagode sosai da aka bani damar fadin albarkacin bakina a cikin wannan shirin mai farin jini zuwa da kai yafi sako. Toh idan muka duba irin tsarin siyasar duniya gaskiya da banbanci da irin namu, dukda tsarin siyasar da muke ba irin na sauran kasashen da suka ci-gaba bane. Sannan idan kika duba yanayin tun daga kan kasar zuwa jahohin mu zaki tabbatar da ana wani irin siyasa ne, mai dauke da rayuwa da mutuwa."

        "Haka ne Dr! Shin wacce irin kallo kakewa Jam'iyyun GDP da PLP, shin wannan adawar da suke yi da juna haka ya dace ko kuma me zaka ce akan haka?"

"Toh Malama Rahama kin tab'a inda ya dace, idan muka dauki tsarin mulkin jahar nan wanda ina da yakinin an rubuta shi tun shekaru ala dari tara da tamanin, (notes babu shi a dokar kasa fa, dan haka a nutsu a karanta labarin ban da bin didigin) shashi na dubu goma da arba'in a sakin layin me code 438, babu inda doka ta bawa wata kungiyar Jam'iyyar damar bin wata kungiyar Jam'iyyar domin cutar da ita, sannan bana Mantawa akwai lokuta da muka yi wata makala Dr Hajji Mukabala tare da Barister Sam'ana Manga akan lallai kotu zata iya yanke hukuncin daurin rai da rai idan aka kama wani dan Jami'ayyar da ya cutar da wata Jami'ayyar. Sannan idan kika duba yanayin siyasar jahar nan kowani shugaba akan tsarin Dr Abdul Hamid Jamil Bagauda yake tafarkin shi ne kuma tsarin shi ake bi.

   Sannan har yau siyasar nan daya ce, wanda shi ya gadar da ita. Sannan kuma duk wata manufar da ake ciki da nuna ta shi ya samar da ita idan muka yi dubi da Yadda ake juya mishi baya gaskiya abin babu dadin ji domin matuƙar aka cigaba da haka toh anan lalata siyasar jahar nan ne, kuma ana karya kashin mutanen akan siyasar. Musamman idan ka duba daga gwamnatin babu abinda suka yi akan matasa amma shi kuwa yayi abinda ba zata ba, yana basa matasa damar baza basirar su."

"Mun gode gashi dai ka dauko bayani amma sabida halin mutumin namu wato lokaci, yana hararan mu sai a da kance mu wani makon idan mai dukka ya nufa zamu sake dawowa a sabon shirin namu me suna. Zuwa da kai yafi sako! Dani mai gabatarwa Rahama Ayman tare da Masanin al'amuran siyasar duniya da a na nan cikin gida Dr Bukar Dalibi"

"Nima nagode sosai da bani damar fashin bakin akan al'amuran siyasar kasar nan"

   ---
"Yallabai me kake tunani akan Dr Bukar Dalibi?"
"Eh toh a wani fuska yayi gaskiya a wani fuskar kuma yana bukatar gayyata ta musamman. Domin idan masana alamarun yau da kullum suka fara fashin bakin akan Dr Abdul Hamid Jamil Bagauda, Bagauda yana kara samun magoya bayan. Jama'a suna tausayin shi sabida ya rasa kafar shi.

         Idan muka Cigaba da takun saka da shi, mune zamu rasa masoyan mu, dan haka karku tanka musu ku Barsu da abinda yake damun su.

   ***
"Wai meye nayi miki ban isa nace a zuba abinci na fita dashi waje ba?  Kawai sai ki shiga fushi da b'acin rai." Ya fada cikin fushi kamar zai dake ta, kuma dukka dukka wata nawa da za a fara jin su. Matsalar Ishrat tana da mugun rowa, suma yan uwan shi suna samun abinta ne sabida Umma tana kyautatta mata, bayan haka d ba zata bawa kowa kome ba.
  Ko abinci zata saka a kai cikin gidan haka zata sakawa Umma kasar kwano, shima dan kar Raees yace bata sakawa mahaifiyar shi abinci ba yada take sakawa.

  Amma ta bangaren Umma Ramla ta jima da fahimtar wacece Ishrat, kuma dake Allah ya bata wadatar zuci sai take ganin ba kome bane. Kuma tana jin a ranta zata yanke kawo abincin baki daya sabida kar haka ta tab'a auren su.   Cikin fushi ya shigo shashin Umma. Ya zauna.
"Umma a zuba abincin da abokaina zamu ci" ya faɗa a sanyayye, duk da a fusace yake amma haka bai hana shi zama mai sanyi a gaban Umma ba,

"Ahmad bana son kana yawan fushi akan wasu, halayyar ka ce kula da harkokin jama'a musamman na unguwar kuka, amma kuma bai dace kana samun matsala da Iyalinka akan mutane ba, idan akan abinci ne na yafe maka kwaryar mu, suma yan wajen kazo ka amsa musu babu amfanin kuyi ta samun matsala akan wasu da basu da iko da rayuwar ka, ita tana da iko da rayuwar ka, ba kamar su ba."

      "Amma"  ta dakatar dashi ga abinda zai fada tana kallon shi..
"Ka zauna lafiya da matarka don Allah karka b'ata mata rai sabida wasu mutane na daban, tunda tana kokarin kyautata maka akan me zaka juya mata baya."

    Nasiha tayi mishi tare da turawa aka kira Ishrat,ta mata nasiha itama. Sannan tace mata.
"Kinga mijinki tun yana sakandiri nake kokarin kara tukunyar girkina sabida shi, Ahmad na jama'a ne, kuma suma jama'ar suna yin shi, dan haka babu laifi zan cigaba da girki dashi amma bana son naji kuna samun matsala dashi, idan kika duba yanayin shi mutum ne da yake iya hakuri ya bawa wasu, don Allah kiyi hakuri dashi, duk da nasan kinayi amma a kara mishi ba zai kuma ba, daga yau na dauke girkin da za a nayi, zan dinga turawa waje, shi kenan."

"Eh babu kome Nagode umma, amma gaskiya ba zan iya hakuri da wannan halin nashi ba, bai da buri sai na wasu bai da cikakken lokaci sai na wasu. Idan nayi magana yafini fushi don Allah ki duba fa, kawai su zauna a waje su ta musu akan siyasa ba zai yi zuciya ya fita niman aiki ba. Shi kenan zanyi hakuri tunda kin ce zaki na girka musu ana kai musu dan wallahi ba zan iya mishi ba, nayi da safe nayi da rana nayi da dare sai kace baiwar su, sannan ga shi."

Umma ta gaji da maganar ta dab haka tace mata.
"Kiyi hakuri, wata rana sai"
"Nagode sosai, Umma." Ta tashi ta bar falon.
Abin mamaki ya bata, tana tausayin Ahmad domin ta Fahimci....

Daga
JARUMAR BOROROJI.

Dr Abdul Hamid Jamil Bagauda.

Lokacin da ya farka ya kalli kafar shi a yanke, wani irin hakuri ne ya shiga ranshi, tare da share wasu tears din da suke zuba, uwar gidan shi mai suna Hajiya Tahirah share kwalla tayi cikin kuka tace mishi.

"Alhaji ka hakura da siyasar nan! Tun yanzun ka fara rasa wani muhimman sassan jikin ka, ina kuma alamarin ya ta'azzara, don Allah ka duba shekarun ka, ka bar siyasan nan haka."

   Murmushi yayi sannan ya dauki madubin shi ya saka, kafin ya juya ya kalli babbar Yar shi yace mata.
"Kin tab'a ganin zaki ya gudu a dawa? Ko kin tab'a tsinkayo wanda ya gujewa mutuwar shi." Yaja wata irin iska, kafin ya fesar sannan ya kalle ta, yace mata.
"Yanzun aka fara, domin ban ci ta zama ba. Ina da goyan bayan al'umma kuma suna mara min baya taya zan yi gudun hijira? Idan mutuwa bibiyata take Tabbas ni ba zan guje mata ba, zan cigaba da bin sawunta dan haka kar na kuma jin maganar ki a cikin al'amarin siyasa ta."

Ganin yadda ya zafaffa al'amarin sai ta zuba mishi ido, domin bata son tashin hankali, kuma bata son abinda zai had'a su. Jama'a da suka fahimci ya farfaɗo suka shiga zuwa duba shi ana girmama al'amarin tare da kammaba. Musamman Yan bani na iya, suka shiga ririta Al'amarin da bai kai a ce an yi tashin hankali akan shi ba, suka fara magana kan cewa.

"Dole muyi bori k'in gwamnatin PLP domin ba zamu zuba ido ana cutar da masu tsaya kare mana hakkin mu ba."

Ai kuwa suka fusata tare da amincewa za a tashi hankalin mutane.
**
"Labaran Duniya, Ke sauraro dani Rahama Ayman, a yau muka samu labarin Mai girma Gwamna ya kafa kwamitin bincike akan harin da aka kaiwa Dr Abdul Hamid Jamil Bagauda, wanda yayi sanadiyar rasa kafar shi daya. Har yanzun kwamitin bata bada wani gamshashen bayani akan yadda zasu tafiyar da binciken su ba.  

       Shugaba kasa Abba Mai nasara ya samu lambar yabo a majalisar dinkin duniya, akan shine shugaba mafi nagarta a yankin Arewacin Afirka. Yan tadda kayar baya, sun samu damar kafa gwamnatin a kasar Balbale, Shugaba Tiriko na kasar Yuno ya rigamu gidan gaskiya, har yanzun ana samun kananun tashin hankali a yankin nahiyar fure. Mu zauna lafiya, sai arziki mai yawa ya shigo jahar mu, wannan sakon daga ofishin sakataren Gwamnatin jaha ta dauka tare da hadin gwiwar matasa masu kishin jahar mu.

  Ana samun bayanin akan cewa talakawa sun shirya zanga zanga akan rasa kafar da dr Abdul Hamid Jamil Bagauda yayi. Anan zauren Zuwa da kai yafi sako muna tare da Comrade Habibu Kani karkashin jagorancin hukumar kare hakkin Dan Adam na duniya, wato human right, sai gefe na akwai brrts Sabo Bako na had'akar kungiyar Bagauda Trust Grp.  Assalamu alaikum, da fatan bakin namu sun iso lafiya."

  "Waalaikumin Salam, Rahama Ayman, yau gamu a cikin shirin naku na zuwa da kai yafi sako."

"Masha Allah! Zamu fara yayye kallabin shirin da Comrade Habibu Kani, shin me kake gani dangane da wannan yunkuri na talakawa kan lallai sai anyi adalci akan abinda aka yiwa Dr Bagauda? Wani kallo kakewa Gwamnati a wannan lokacin?"

"Alhamdulillahi, kin tabo inda yake min kaikayi, amma kuma ba zai hana mu magana ba, Abinda talakawa suka fito yi lallai haka shine Alkhairi, domin suna da damar fita niman hakkin su.  Idan akace yau wani ya tsaya miki idan kika yi kuka shi zai budewa duniya suji kukanki, ai sai san Barka, toh an wayi gari mai tsaya miki ya koma gefe a matsayin ki na kishi da kishin sa ai dole ki fusata akan gwamnatin nan, sannan a yadda abin ya faru, kowa yasan yaran nan yan Sara suka ne, kuma suka ce lallai idan nai fito ba zasu kona ofishin jami'ayyar, wanda yake dauke da mutane babu adadi ai tashin hankalin yayi yawa, dan haka Dattijon arziki, Mutumin kirki. Ya sadaukar da rayuwar shi domin al'umma dole ran talaka ya b'aci sannan.."

"Comrade Habibu Kani, ban katse maka hanzari ba, bari muji abinda Brrst zai ce akan dokar kasa, Barister ko akwai abinda dokar kasa tace akan kundin tsarin walwalar jama'a?"

Cikin gurbatacciyar Hausar shi da bata zauna akan harshen shi ba yace.
"Toh Rahmah Ayman,a dokar jahar nan dai an bawa kowa damar ya gudanar da abinda yake so! Sannan doka bata bawa kowa daman cin zarafin wani ba, sannan suma ta ɓangaren gwamnati ya kamata su kama yaran nan su hukunta shi, domin ko jiya naga duguja da Uban zuga, idan har irin su zasu kaiwa Babban mutum mai daraja haka farmaki meye mu kuma zamu ce?

    Ana gani ana ji an sare Dr Bagauda har sai da aka cire mishi kafar shi me talakawa zasu iya, fatana guda daya ne! Talakawan da zasu fito zanga zanga don Allah kar gwamnatin jaha ta cutar dasu domin suna kaunar Dr Abdul Hamid Jamil Bagauda"

   "Comrade Habibu Kani,shin wani tsari hukumar kare hakkin dan Adam, na karkashin kungiyar ku ta ce akan haka wai nufin akwai wani abu da za a iya musu ne?" Inji Rahama Ayman,

  "Toh alhakika mun samu damar tura sakon mu zuwa babban Ofishin mu ta kasa, ita kuma ta tura zuwa babban Ofishin mu ta duniya baki daya, sannan a taron da za ayi na Afrika Insha Allah muna saka ran za'a taso da maganar nan, idan baku manta ba Dr Bagauda ya rike member ba majalisar dinkin duniya, sannan yayi aikin diplomatic na tsawon shekaru goma, ya zauna a kwamitin sulhu na Majalisar Dinkin Duniya, sannan ya shiga hadakar kungiyar sa kai na kasashen Afrika zuwa nahiyar Turai. Dr Bagauda ya jagoranci wata tawagar zuwa kasar Hura, akan zaman lafiya.

    Dr Abdul Hamid Jamil Bagauda, ya jagoranci yankin kasashen Larabawa akan rigimar su da taki ci taki cinyewa, Dr Bagauda mutum ne masanin al'amuran siyasar duniya, kuma anan kasar mu ma, ya jagoranci yankunan karkara akan  Cigaba da inganta musu rayuwa, ya taimakawa matasa dari biyar, suka dogara da kansu, Dr Abdul Hamid Jamil Bagauda ya bawa ya'yan kaji hatsi sunci sun share bakin su, kai har yau salon siyasar shi ce ake gudanar."

     "Toh gashi ka dibo bayani amma lokaci abokin tafiya sai hararanmu yake, Dani Rahama Ayman tare da Comrade Habibu Kani da Barister Sabo bako. Muke muku Barka da rana daga gidan Radio gama gari FM."

   ***
"Hmm" inji Nuhu, lokacin da ya jefa kosai da bread a bakin shi yana gyada kai, Raees da yake kallon shi.
"Adam baka ji abinda ake fada ba ne?" Inji Nuhu,
"Toh kai Nuhu ban da abinka me yasa ba zaka fadi abinda yake ranka ba?"

"Toh amma wallahi indai Mutum irin Dr Abdul Hamid Jamil Bagauda zai ajiye hidimar shi ya fada siyasa wallahi babban cigaba ne, amma kaga yadda aka butulce mishi." Inji Nuhu,

Murmushi Adam yayi bayan ya tsiyayyi tea yana kallon Raees da idanun shi yake lumshe kamar yana barci.

"Mai girma Shugaban kasa, baka ce kome na?"

Murmushi yayi domin idan Adamu yana son tsokanar shi haka yake gaya mishi, Nuhu kuma yace mishi Honorable.

"Bana sha'awar siyasa amma har yau zuciyata tana kwadaita min son haduwa da Dr Abdul Hamid Jamil Bagauda."

    "Kai Haba?"
"So nake nagan shi sosai na fahimci me yasa ake mishi butulci ne haka?"

Dariya suka saka mishi, sannan Nuhu yace mishi.
"Mutumina idan ka shiga siyasa wannan shegen fadin ranka da son mulkinka sai ya kara hauhawa kamar furashin Gwauron zabi."

Murmushi yayi sai da dimple point din shi ya lubba sannan yace musu.
"Allah yayi min tsari, ban hango hadiri ba, taya zan bata jikina da kashi? Ai wannan lamari sai na masu yanci. Ni da Unguwar mu ma dakyar nake iya tankwara yaran unguwar balle mulki baki daya kai ko siyasar bana fatar shigar ya ɓalle mulkin kai manta kawai."

    Zuwan Zakaria ne yace mishi.
"Dan iska idan ka shiga siyasa wallahi Mantawa zaka yi da mun tab'a zama a kofar gidan ku, Umma ta girka mana abin karyawa. Domin kuwa yan siyasa ba daraja ce pda ku ba"

Ya fada tare da ɗaukar kofi ya cika da ruwan zafi, sannan ya jefa lipton ya dauko sugar me iyali yana zubawa yace.
"Dan Umma kai dai Allah ya baku lada, amma wallahi yau kwandala bana maganin shi, kai ma kenan da kayi karatun bokon baka zauna a banza ba, kana tafiya saro bakin mai, amma mu gashi bamu ga tsuntsu bamu ga tarko ga dan banzan tara iyalai da nayi kuma, wallahi yanzun haka Zinnirah ciki ne da ita bata yaye Takwaran ka ba, abin haushi yaron watan shi goma sha biyar fa. Umma Hani kuwa dama ita kam ba haihuwar take akai akai na, sai dai Murja itama ko yau ko gobe wallahi na rasa yadda zan yi da rayuwata,yadda kasan na gudu na bar garin....

Daga
JARUMAR BOROROJI.

Ahmad Bala Raees
Tashi Raees yayi daga zaune ya kura mishi ido, kafin yace mishi.
"Kai ko dan dabarbarun nan ba kayi ne na niman abin rufa asiri?" Ya tambaye shi tare da kafa mishi manyan idanun shi nan masu matukar kyau da girma dan har wani maiko maiko suke yi. Ya zubawa Zakaria,

"Wallahi Raees ban san me zanyi ba, kuma kasan ina dan tab'a harkan siyasa kuma shi din ma ba ko yaushe ake samun abinda ya dace ba, indai ba Maula zaka je yi ba ko farfaganda. Toh nan ne zaka samu abin hasafi, kuma muna saka ran idan har Jam'iyyar GDP ta amshi mulkin jahar nan zamu huta Tabbas."

   Cike da mamaki Raees yake kallon shi kafin yace mishi.
"Yanzun kai har sai ka jira gawon shanu? Yaushe zaka saka rai akan abin da bai zama mallakinka na, icce mai kama ake kota, taya zaka ji sara baka duba bakin gatarin ka ba, karka zama daya daga cikin mutanen da basu gane lissafin yau balle na gobe, duk wanda kaji an kira shi wane kafin wannan zamanin sai da yayi aiki tukuru, na zata kallona da kuke zai zaburar da ku ashe babu amfanin zama da irin ku, tunda na zaku amfanar da kanku ba, akan me zan zauna da ku."

Ya fada tare da kallon Salim kanin shi da ya sauka akan mashin.
"Lafiya?"
Ya wurga mishi tambaya, cikin dinbun damuwa yace mishi.
"Ya Raees wallahi mu dai talakawa mun b'ata rawan mu da tsalle!"

Tattaro duk nutsuwar shi yayi tare da kura mishi ido.
"Ina jinka" ya kuma cewa,
"Wai zanga zanga, suka shiga kawai dan abinda ya faru da Dr Bagauda, kuma jarabawa muke fa"
"Eh ban ga laifin shi ba, domin mutum kamar shi me kawo cigaba a cikin jahar mu dole ayi domin a nunawa gwamanti kuskuren ta."
Inji Adam da yake fada, nuna  mishi hanyar gida yayi.
"Ka ga shi ya dawo a fusace ne akan sanadin da aka mishi na rasa jarabawar shi, amma ko shi nayi imani da Allah yana kaunar dr Abdul Hamid Jamil Bagauda, mu bar saka kanen mu cikin al'amarin siyasa, tunda ba wai gadar mana aka yi ba, Ni ne gaji da jin haukar su." Ya fada tare da mik'ewa zai nufi gurin bakin man shi.

"Raees ban san ya aka yi jikina yake bani wani abu na daban akan ba, amma tabbas kai din na musamman ne" inji Zakaria,

  Murmushi yayi sannan ya gama abinda zai yi ya juya tare da cewa.
"Bari na turo Salim, zan mike."
"Mara mutunci" inji Nuhu,
"Daman bai da ita" bai saurare su ba, yayi shigewar shi. Tare da nufa dakin Ishrat, da take kwance Khadijah tana mata wanke wanke.
Kallonta yayi sannan ya zauna.
"Yau ba zaki daura abinci bane?" Ya tambaye ta tana kallon Film din da take kallo na dawo dawo,
"Wallahi na gaji, ko zaka gayawa Umma ta taimaka mana da abincin rana ne, kasan jiya tunda ka dawo baka saurara min ba."

         Shafa sajen shi yayi sannan yace mata.
"Umma ba zata yi girki akai waje kuma ta zuba min ni dake ba, idan zaki tashi ki tashi domin zan kwanta ne"

                Tashi tayi tare da tura baki tana kallonshi.
"Ni zan je na gaya mata ai"
"Jeki idan baki da kunya ke na lura da rashin kunyar ki a fili take, matar da kike jin ba dad'i zaki saka mata lomar tuwo shine zaki je gaya mata ta saka abincin dake, hmm baki da kunya sam"

     Fuuuu ta shige d'akinta, shima ta gyara kwanciyar shi a dogon kujeran shi.

     Ko minti talatin bai da fara barci ba, hayaki me zafi ya cika mishi hanci da wuyar shi.
"Lafiya" yaga Ishrat can kwance ta suma, da sauri ya d'aga ta, tare da jijjigata, da sauri ya fito tare da leka kofar gida babu kowa, sai sojojin da suke ta zuba tighgas. Ga shagon bakin man shi bude, dan haka ya fito cikin nutsuwa.

"Kai Mahaukacin ina ne?"
"Officer shagona yana buɗe dan haka ban isa na shiga na zauna bayan shagon shi yake rufa min asiri na" ya fada tare da fitowa baki daya.
"Idan ka fito zamu harbe?"
"I know my worth so I will not sit idly by, dan haka ba zan zauna nayi asarar kayana ba" ya fada tare da isa bakin shagon shi ya rufe shagon, sannan ya juya ya nufi cikin gidan shi." Ya rufe kafin ya koma kan Ishrat ya zuba mata ruwa, zafin hayakin tighgas ne ya sumar da ita. Bayan ta farka ya bata ruwa ta sha, cikin gidan ya shiga ta kofar baya.
"Umma kuna lafiya?"
"Eh lafiya, yanzun nake yiwa Salim fada akan me zai shigo bai rufe shagon ka ba, ai kamar na dake shi."

"Ya Raees kafin mu farga kawai muka ga sojojin akan mu yaushe zamu bi takan shagunan mu?"
Zama yayi tare da basu labarin ta rufe shagon ai, amma sai da suka yi tankiya da sojojin tukun. Khadeeja da take manne jikin Umma, sai zare ido take, shigowar Ishrat kamar wacce tayi jinya yasa Umma Rudewa.
"Lafiya?"
"Suma tayi sabida shaƙar iskar tighgas ne ta koma haka"

    Abin ba ya fahimta a zaman Ishrat a falon tana son tambayar abinci ne, ya fahimci haka ne ta yadda take kallon kolar abincin da ake kaiwa waje.

"Umma ba dai har kin gama abinci ba?"
"Eh na gama wallahi za a fitar dashi fa aka fara tashin hankalin nan, sai ku dauka ku kai gurinku kuci" boyayyen ajiyar zuciya ya sauke tare da kallonta.
"Gaskiya kin gama mana kome, domin yadda wannan matsoraciya take zare ido bana tunanin zata iya yarda ta moru"

   Cikin jin kunya tace.
"Umma kin ganshi ko? Murmushi Umma tayi sannan tace.
"Ku tafi ku bani guri na gaji da surutun ku, ni ban san ya zanyi da kamu na ba, domin na jika shi sai dai mu canza ruwan geron amma Tabbas an saka mu cikin tashin hankali."

"Sun saka dokar tab'aci ne?"
"Eh Ya Raees curfew aka saka na 24hrs"

"Toh wai zanga zanga ce ta ta'azzara ce haka?"
"Eh wallahi abin ya koma fada tsakanin yan jam'iyyar PDP da Yan Jam'iyyar GDP, kafin kace kwabo har an fara kona wasu ma'aikatun gwamnati, kuma abin haushi wai fadar har ya shafi talakawa fa? Kuma sai da aka ce ban da ɗaukar makami a fita zanga-zangar."

     "Toh Allah ya kawo mana sauki da wannan tashin hankali."

Haka suka dauki kular abincin su, suka musu sallama, ya garin ya koma shiru.

Bayan awa ashirin da huɗu,

Kai tsaye aka sake hoton mai girma Gwamna.

"Ina kiran yan jahar mu, su kwantar da hankalin su. Su daina barnata dukiyar gwamnati, yin haka babban laifi ne da asara ba, idan muka duba dukkan mu Abu daya ne, taya zamu na b'ata dukiyar gwamnati. Bayan muna sane da dayawa bashi muke ciwuwa daga ƙasashen waje, dan haka duk wanda aka kama da laifin koma wani gurin zamu yanke mishi hukuncin zama gidan yari na wata shida da tara mai yawa. Zaman lafiya yafi zaman dan Sarki."

Ka tsaye daga gidan tv1 aka sake wannan lamarin.

      Kashe tv yayi yana kallonta yau ma babu alamar zata yi girki.
"Ke meye nufinki? Abun karyawa a gurin Umma na amso mana yanzun ma abincin ranar ba zaki yi ba?" Ya tambaye ta,

"Bana jin dadi ne! Kuma kai ma Raees wallahi baka da tausayi, tsakanin da Allah, da dare ka nime Ni sau biyu, da asuba ma kafin ka tafi masalaci ma haka, da safen nan ma zan yi abun karyawa kace nazo ni, wallahi ka mai dani kamar wata injin."

     Kura mata ido yayi sannan ya tashi tare da fita ya barta, wai a haka ma yana ƙoƙarin nimo abincin da zasu ci, duk tsiya karya kazo gidan babu abincin musamman Umman shi,ya rasa wacce irin mace ce Ishrat, bata son tabuwa, sau dari idan zai nime ta, sai tayi ƙorafi, kuma duk ƙoƙarin shi da ya kyautata mata, amma sam bata gani aure ko shekara biyu ba ayi ba sai tarin damuwa da takaici.

Dr Abdul Hamid Jamil Bagauda.

2013

Aishafareeda.

Kaunar da nakewa turare ya b'aci, domin ina ganin damar na shafa Kwalba guda, kuma duk kyautar da za a bani matukar bata turare bane aikin banza ce.
     Duk abinda zaka min matukar zaka bani turare zan amsa tare da godiya.

   Had'a kayana nake ina kuka tare da tattara kayan turarena.

"Aishafareeda kiyi hakuri, Insha Allah duk Shekara zaki na zuwa hutu, saura shekara biyu ki gama karantun ki, idan kika kammala zaki zo hutu. Ita kanta Hajiya Turai bata da lafiya, kewar zai mata yawa shi yasa muka yanke hukuncin ki koma gurinta kar son kai yayi yawa."

      Juyawa nayi na fashe da kuka, tare da fadawa jikin kakata, kawu Aashir ne ya shigo yana faɗin.
"Tun jiya ake cewa kin had'a kaya  , ashe sakarcin ki ne har yanzu. Maza ki fito  ina jiranki"

    Haka aka Mama ta had'a min kayan kamshina, tare da zuge jakar.  Aka shiga fitar da kayan kusan jakkuna biyar na kayana.

Sai katon jaka na turaren kayana na fara kuka, ina sanye da abaya pink, sai mayafin da na yane kaina.

Kasa zuwa gurin Hajja Aliyah nayi bayan na shiga motar kawu Aashir, na kifa kaina sai kuka har muka bar gidan, ya kawo ni airport inda mutumin da zai kawo ni garin mu yake jirana.

Ina fita kawu Aashir ya riko hannuna har gurin mutumin, yace mishi.
"Sha'aban ga yar kanwata, don Allah ka mikata ga Hajiya Turai, ga amanarta nan na baka"

   "Insha Allah, Aashir zan kai ta har gida Nagode sosai da yarda da kayi dani."

Haka suka gama godiya da fatan Allah ya kai mu lafiya, Kawu na tsaye muka shiga jirgin.

     Juyawa yayi tare da share kwalla, domin shima ya saba da Aishafareeda.

***
  Karfe bakwai.
Na dare muka sauka a babban birnin garin,  shi ya fito min da kayan mu, har inda motar gidan shi ya zo daukar shi.

        Kaf shida driven shi duka kwashe kayan, suka zuba motar har zuwa inda zan zauna kayana ne, kallon shi drvn yayi sannan yace mishi.
"Yallabai ban da Chad ka fito da nace ko daga Maiduguri kake irin wannan kamshin haka?"

Murmushi yayi sannan ta juya ya ga hankalina yana kallon hanyar.
"Aishafareeda ce take sanye da shi,kasan Zuri'ar Aashir mai turare ne,yanzun ka kai mu Unguwar kuka, farkon layin."

"Insha Allah ba zaki tab'a samu na a cikin mutane masu kai wa da komowa ba, na miki alƙawarin haka."

  Sannan yayi musu sallama, ya fita.
--
Ina kwance naji Hajiya na salati, da sauri na fito.
"Lafiya?"
"Wai an Sara suka sun kaiwa makarantar ku shara, sannan sun fitar da Umar suna gana mishi azaba" gyada kai nayi tare da jin wani irin abu yana hawa kaina, dakin na shiga na cire doguwar rigar jikina, na saka riga da wando, sannan na daura After dress akan shi da mayafi na fita bayan na saka boot, na fita.
"Don Allah Karku barta ta fita."

Kafin wani ya rike Ni na fita ina zan kulata, da gudu nake ratsa unguwar makarantar mu. A bakin get na tsaya bayan na shiga . Yana kwance a filin makarantar, numfashi nake saukewa tare da nufar cikin makarantar, zuciyana yana tafasa. Ina zuwa na d'ago shi ina kallon su, suka taso.
"Ke yar iska ke muke nima," taka dutse nayi tare da murza shi.

Kafin na durkusa cikin zafin nama na saita gwiwar shi na hagu, na kai mishi wani.irin jifa sai da ya fadi.

Cikin sauri Umar yake miko min dutse ina fashe musu kai. Sai gashi da iya duwatsu na Kore su a cikin makarantar. Da sauri shugaban makarantar yazo tare da cewa.
"Mun gode! Ba dan ke ba, bamu san me zasu mishi ba, mun gode." A hankali na kamo hannun shi, na zaunar dashi. Kafin na nufi ajin su na dauko jakar shi zan fita, wani yaro yace min.
"Yar daba kawai" juyawa nayi na kifa mishi mari! Sai da ajin ya amsa.
"Kul ka kirani da sunan nan?" Bai iya magana ba, sai bayan na fita wani a cikin ajin yace mishi.
"Deen hancinka yana jini" shafawa yai tare da kallon yadda na riko hannun Umar.
"Shegiyar yarinya wallahi zaka dauka ko kera mata hannun aka yi kamar ba na mace ba"
Muna fitowa Hajiya suna isowa da mai kawo mu, saka shi nayi muka nufi asibitin. Anan aka mishi duk abinda zasu iya mishi suka sallame mu zuwa gida.

   **
Hajiya fada tai min bayan ta gama ta buge da rarrashina, tun daga ranar muka sami hutu da matsalar yan iska a unguwar mu. Sai dai karfi da yaji ana niman manna min hauka a cikin unguwar mu, ba dan kome ba sai dan yadda suka zuba min ido. Kowa da abinda yake fada a kaina da na Alkhairi da na sharri.
Irin haka ya fada kunnen Hajiya bata wani damu ba, musamman da ake yawan yabon Aishafareeda, sai dai haka ya tab'a rayuwar yarinyar sabida yadda ake yawan magana akan kyanta da kuma karfin halinta.

    ***
A cikin wannan yanayin zab'e ya karato, aka fara zazzabar kamfen,abinda ya tab'a rayuwar al'umma kenan, a cikin su har dani domin ina kaunar naga an fita rally, har kin zuwa makarantar nake, na fara bin madam Ruth, idan zan tafi makarantar sai na wuce gidanta.

   Koda aka ganni aka gayawa Hajiya bata Zafaffa ba, ita babban damuwar ta, kar na zubda mutuncina da kuma darajata, sai karatuna. Domin lokacin da ta sami labarin ta min faɗa kuka na saka tare da cewa.
"Idan Abbana yana raye ba zai takura min ba amma ke duk abinda aka dauko aka gaya miki dauka kike, shi kenan zan koma chad" jin haka yasa ta shiga rarrashina tare da gaya min wanda ya kawo gulmar, ni kuwa nace ya gama zaman gidan haya a gidan baki daya.

    "Ayya Sharubutun mai turaren, ayi haka? Koyi hakuri a barshi Insha Allah ba zan Kuma ba, na miki alƙawarin bani kuma ɗaukar maganar wani."  Ta fada min, a hankali naga yana share kwalla tare da rike hannuna.
"Wallahi ba zan hanaki abinda kike so ba" kwalla suka ci-gaba da zuba a idanun ta.

"Kinsan ke mace ce, kuma duk abinda zaki yi karki rasa mutuncin ki, idan kika min haka wallahi wallahi kin gama min kome, ba zan kuma ɗaukar maganar mutane ba, amma ki kammala karatun ki sai kiyi duk abinda zaki yi"

   Ta bani tausayi domin nasan da iyayena suna raye ba zasu Barta tayi kuka haka ba. Gashi Ni karama dani na sata kuka.

"Hajiyana Insha Allah, ba zan tab'a zubda darajar gidan nan ba."
         Tun daga ranar rashin mutunci ya kafu a gidan mu, tare da dokoki kala kala, ba a kunna radio idan ina barci, ba a wasa ko hayaniya, haka yasa wasu zagina tare da gulmata.

     Akwai wata mata da suke haya, mai sunan Sugura, matar bata da mutunci, haka ta ishe mu ita da Yaranta Yan mata.  Sai na dawo daga yawo na zasu kama min habaici. Ban cika kula su ba, domin ina jin hajiyayye tana musu magana da uwar Yaran.

"Wallahi Maman Jamilah ki daina abinda kike aikatawa, gidan nan da kike zaune kakar Yarinyar nan ne take dashi, idan wani abu yarinyar ta miki sai ki gayawa kakar balle kuma ita kanta Yarinyar bata da matsala matukar ka fahimci halinta, ba ki zauna ki ta zaginta da mata habaici ba, kina da yara mata uku Jamila ta girme ta, Nana sa'arta ga kuma Zakiya, itama basu wuce kai ɗaya ba.

     Idan kika sake Hajiya Turai taji ranki zai b'aci, wallahi kuma ai uwa ta gari bata da bakin zagin dan wani"

   "Toh a karanbana, Uwar Muminai na gidan Hajiya Turai, ban da son zuciya ya kina da budurwa a gabanki Na'ilah, kuma nema kin san yarinyar nan ko da iya kyanta aka bar su, babu mashin shin da zai zo gurin yaran mu, shegiyar yarinyar sai kace aljana ko wani irin kwalliya tayi sai ya karbe ta, Insha Allah a yawon ta zubar zata kare rayuwar ta. Abin kunya kuwa idan da nisan kwana sai ta ajiyewa Hajiya shi"

   Ta fada tare da buga hannunta, idan na saka aka kore ta, ban burge ba. Amma idan na barta ta zauna taga yadda Ubangiji yake ikon shi yafi sauki. Dan haka nayi kamar ban ji ba, daga Chad ana min aika kuma ina kaiwa Makarantar mu,  Mama ma tana sayarwa,

                  Lokacin da Umar ya gama karatun shi, Hajiya ta shiga niman Kawu Sha'aban shi ya yi mishi kome aka tura shi, Sudan.

    Dama sabida takurani da yake yi ne da magana yasa bana son fito da halina, yana barin kasar. Na yanke zuwa makaranta, hatta sana'ar sayar da turaren a yawon bangan siyasa na ke yin shi. Gani yarinya me karamin shekaru, amma wallahi ko barkono ya sallama mini, kuma haka ya kara min daraja a gurin Madam Ruth.

     Ita da kanta ta kaini Office din Alhaji Sani Musa, shugaban jam'iyyar PLP, ya kare min kallo sannan ya ce ta fita. Tana fita ya dawo gabana ya zauna.
"Maraba da zuwa sashen mu, lokacin da nasaka aka kawo ki baki ce kome ba, gashi nan kin kuma dawowa nan."

    Mikewa nayi tare da cewa.
"Kai baka da matsala ne? Dan haka sai an jima" na juya tare da barin shi a tsaye, zuciyar shi  cike da farin cikin. Abinda yake dako ya zo har gidan shi, nasarar shi zata bayyana.

"Gani yake a lokacin bamu da hankali, sannan bamu san me muke ba, kullum shine mai lafiyar da zai kwanta da mace sau uku zuwa hudu a dare guda, shi wannan al'amarin ba haka yake ba, idan har kana da macen da kake so! Kana kaunarta toh dole ka bukace ta, musamman kayi dace da wacce zata iya daukar nauyin ka, ta kuma san darajar ka, toh ba makawa zaka iya biyar da ita sau bakwai ma a dare guda, ba wai dan kawai kana samun yan canji zaka dauko mata ka aura ba, ai abinda ya kashe na gidan shi zai kashe wacce zaka aura, shi yasa nake tattalin iyalina" inji Adamu.

"Amma ku munafukai ne? Duk cikinku babu wanda ya tab'a zaunar da Zakaria ya gaya mishi gaskiya abinda yake faruwa da shi ba, amma kun zauna kuna gulmar shi, wallahi yayi kokari da ya tara mata, kuma yake zaune lafiya dasu, kuma wallahi da yana da Wadata babu wanda ya isa yayi gulmar shi, ku daina haka haramun ne, zaman tare ya wuce haka, duk inda zaka zauna da mutane ka zauna dasu da gaskiya da amana."

   Kamar wanda ruwa yayi musu duka haka suka sunkuyar da kai, sannan ya Cigaba da abinda yake domin shi dai bai da matsala dasu. Wata yarinya ce tazo ta wuce da wasu irin kananun kaya, zunzurutun ya matse ta, har kana iya hango mazauninta, kiran sunan Allah yake, kafin yaji ina ba zai iya zama ba, kawai ya nufi gidan shi, dariya suka saka mishi.
"Za'a tafi ban ruwa ne" banza yayi dasu ya shige a hankali bai damu da dariyar shu'umancin da suke mishi ba, shi baya cikin jerin mutanen da suke da mugun bukata, amma idan da zai ga koda sumbata ce, a Film take jinin shi yake harbawa, balle kuma taga mace ta wuce ta gaban shi, da guda yake barin shafin shi.
Yana shiga ya samu Ishrat ta fito da wanki tana yi, sai lokacin ya tuna da ashe sun yi faɗa kafin ya fita. Kuma idan suka samu irin wannan sab'ani hana shi hakkin shi take, dan haka yana shiga bai tsaya rokonta ba, daukarta yayi suka wuce cikin falo, zata mishi tijara, ya had'e bakin su guri guda, kafin ya shiga aika mata da makaman nukiliyar shi.. Kafin kace me, tayi laushi jikin shi yana rawa ya rufe kofar falon, ya dawo sannan ya shiga shafeta. Tare da tsutseta, duk ta cika mishi kune da Moaning, a hankali ya zauna bayan ya d'agota ya direta a saman raees din shi, cikin wani irin kara ta sake ajiyar zuciya, shima kuma haka, tare da rungumar ta, yana jin saukar Ni'imarta, a saman raees din shi, wayyo a zauce yake binta kamar zai cinye ta danye, bai samu kanshi ba, sai da yayi bayin shi son ranshi, kuma ya lura da ita idan ba irin wannan  abun ya mata ba, sai ya gama bata kawo kai ba. Matsa kirjinta yayi yana kallon ta, yadda take sauke numfashin ta.
"Tashi muyi wanka"
Haka ya d'ago ta, sannan ya nufi hanyar ban daki da ita.

***
"Tabbas akwai abubuwa masu tsada a cikin wannan yanayin, mafi alkhairi da girman shi, ka mallaki abinda ya zartas su. Karka sake ayi zaben nan baka makamar iko, ka zartas su iko da fadar aji. Dole sai ka mallaki shafukan da babu rubutu a cikin su, dama na gaya maka zaka dawo, toh wannan shine tafiyar farko da wancan da wannan sune  karfin ka, karka yarda wani ya tsaya maka ba tare da ka samu wadannan taurari biyun ba.

   Saura wata guda, kuma al'amari ne kara rincabewa, dan haka kayi maza ka samo min yadda za ayi."

"Toh sai"
"Tashi ka tafi, a fara niman Almajirai makafi maza bakwai mata bakwai, a binne su bayan an musu yankan rago, a nimo min budurwan karya, sannan a kawo min matashin zaki a kawo min shi nan akwai aikin da zan yi dashi"

Gyada kai yayi sannan ya mike tare da ajiye jakar kudin ya fita abin shi.

   **
Cikin lokaci ƙalilan aka fara hidimar zab'e, inda aka fara da na shugaban kasa, bayan an yi an gama aka fitar da sanarwa wanda ya lashe zaben shugaban kasa.
Sati biyu a tsakanin aka kuma gudanar da zaben gwamnonin Kasar baki daya tare da na yan majalisu tarayya da na jahohin kasar baki.

Ranar da za ayi zab'en kafa kujera nayi na zauna a bakin titin unguwar mu, ina zaune aka yi parking da gabana.

Sauke glass din motar yayi tare da cewa.
"Ke me kike yi anan?" Ganin yayi min kwarjini ya sani mik'ewa nace mishi.
"Ina zaune ne sabida masu kawowa yan zab'e hari?"
"Waye ya saki?"
"Madam Ruth!" Na fada.
Sauka yayi daga motar tare da saukowa da sandar shi da yake dogarawa. Murmushi yayi sannan yace min.
"Baki jin tsoro ne?"
"A'a bana jin haka"
"Ya sunanki?"
"Aishafareeda Hassan!"
"Kece Uwar daban unguwar nan ko?"
"Hmm" nace mishi.
"Shekarun ki nawa?"
"Goma sha shida"
"Me yasa ba zaki koma makaranta ba?"
"Shekara me zuwa zanyi candy"
"Yanzun idan aka kawo harin me zaki yi?"
"Eh toh ba mamaki dan kana sauri ne amma Yan unguwar sun san wacece Ni"

"Siyasa ya dauke min kafana, daya. Nine Alhaji Abdul Hamid Jamil Bagauda" kallon shi nayi domin ina jin sunan shi.
"Me yasa kika zab'i zama da Jam'iyyar PLP! Mu yan jam'iyyar GDP muna tare da gaskiya da amana Insha ALLAH ba zamu ci amanar ki ba, ki saka ido akan zab'en mu yan GDP Bama tashin hankali da hargitsi.

  Na baki damar zama Agent din mu, na yarda da nagartan ki, idan muka samu mazaban unguwar kuka baki daya, dake zamu yi alfahari idan muka fadi ke zamu yi kuka dake.

    Sannan daga yau ba zaki kuma zama karamar uwar Daban ba, sai dai Babbar uwar Daba, na garin nan baki daya. Zan maidake Hannun Damana, kuma zan baki damar kiyi rayuwar da kike so, kawai ki Cigaba da nima mana mata da matasa, zaki iya?"

Murmushin gefen baki nayi tare da cewa.
"Ban tab'a saka abu na fadi ba, kodan na nuna maka wacece ni zan baka mamaki "

"Zan so ba ga haka kuwa"

   Ya juya tare da shiga motar shi. Ya Cigaba da tafiya cikin unguwar. Ban tab'a sanin Ni kaina hatsabibiya bace sai da na zaga baki daya Unguwar na tabbatar Jami'ayyar GDP ne tayi nasara,.kuma naji mutane suna fatan Alkhairi akan Jam'iyyar. Haka ya kara min kwarin gwiwar tsaya musu. Kuma Alhamdulillahi domin anyi nasara.

        Bayan awa ashirin da hudu ka tabbatar zaben gwamna a jahar mu, PLP ce ta lashe, amma tun daga kan muƙamin kansila har zuwa yan majalisu baki daya GDP ce taci, mafi kyau da nagartan zabe shine wanda aka yi shi a unguwar kuka.  Ina barcin Hajiya ta buga min kofa, dakyar na tashi.

     "Kizo garin shegen rashin jin ki na zab'e kin gayyato mana wani can ana kiranki!". After dress na daura akan kayan barcina, na fito a hankali.

Sabuwar mota ce gal a laida, ina fitowa aka mika min.
"Inji Dr Bagauda!" Wai idan kin gama ki shigo yau kwai walima da za ayi da karfe biyu na rana. Akwai sako a cikin Motar."

  A hankali na fita tare da murza Idanuna, kasa magana nayi na juya gida da gudu ban amshi key motar Ba, na shiga dakina turare na kwaso ban sani ba ko zai saka, ina fitowa na mika mishi.

"Bani da kudi sai naje Bank, ga wannan don Allah ka amsa karamin kyauta ce! Insha Allah idan muna tare zan maka babban kyautawa." 

"Nagode sosai!"
Shima yace min, Hajiya da tafito jin ina ihun murna, addu'o'in ta shiga musu, tare da Fatah alkhairi. Bude bayan motar nayi na ciro wata jaka babba, sannan na shiga cikin gidan mu, ina murmushi.
"Wallahi sai ni Aishafareeda ba gaba da Ba gaba da gaba ba, wallahi ko ta baya sai an shirya."

      Babu wanda yayi magana, da na shiga dakin, warware kayan nayi gown ce Royal blue color sai takalmi da yar pose. Gyara zamana tare da farin ciki.

  Aikuwa gidan mu aka maida abinda na samu gulma, duk da wasu na fadawa kunnen Hajiya, wasu kuwa bata ji, haka ya saka ta kara dagewa da min addu'o'in. 

Ni kuwa kamar daka jiran haka nake, na kuma fantsamawa. Domin naje walimar nasarar Jam'iyyar GDP, lokacin da aka muka zo domin mutumin da ya kawo min motar shi yaxo ya dauke ni, ina jin Iyami tana cewa.
"Barin gado asara."
"Eh ai gwara mu gadon bangan siyasa muka yi, wasu muma gadon sata suka yi" na gaya mata, ina jin Maman Jamilah tana faɗin.
"Da alamu Hajiya Turai zata goyi dan gaba da fatiha?"

Har na fita na dawo da sauri.
"Ba zata goya ba, amma ke da alamu zuwa chemist din Uchanna ya zama tsohon yanayi, tunda daga babban yatsar zuwa na tsakiyar mika gindin su, suke ana jagwalgwalawa. Ni kuwa iya aiki daya naci babbar mota, ya kike ganin idan zan mika gindina. Ai kullum ina hanyar dubai da London, su da suke iyawa bawa yan garuwa gindi kiyi nazari mana ni da yan siyasa nake gogayya."

    Daga haka na fito, ashe sun kure Hajiya tace mata.
"Sugura! Tattara kayan ku, na gaji da abinda kike min"

    "Don Allah Hajiya ki rufa min asiri, wallahi bani da kudin da zan iya biyan wani gidan hayar wannan ma alfarma kika min har yau ban gama biyan wanda yake kaina ba"

"Iyama kema ki bar gidan nan na gaji" inji Hajiya,

Hankalin su ya tashi dole suka nimo Baba Sallau, shi ya bawa Hajiya hakuri, dakyar ta amince.

  Kuma da sharadin babu ruwan su dani, kuma haka kar ta kuma gani ko ji.

      ***
Anyi taron ban mamaki, kuma Dr Bagauda wanda nake kira Daddy, ya gabatar dani akan hannun damar shi, mutane dayawa sun yi farin ciki, na samu kyauta dayawa, sabida aikin da nayi.

      Haka muka taso karfe biyar aka kawo ni gida, a mota na. Bayan na dawo Hajiya ta gaya min yadda suka yi share su nayi, domin bani da lokacin su.

     **
Bayan wata shida,

Duk wanda ya san Aishafareeda idan ya ganta yanzun yasan ta wuce sanin shi, kaf unguwar babu me iya tsayawa a gaban ta yayi magana, domin daurin gindin da ta samu a gurin manyan yan siyasa, da matan su.

   Yawo da suke da Bagauda sai Ubangiji, idanunta yayi masifar budewa, baki daya tazama kamar ƙaramar karuwa, babu abinda ya dame ta, idan ta shiga guri kafin a kira sunan ta, kamshin turaren ta ya gama  gaya maku wacece a gurin.

   **
"Ruth me yasa baki gaya min cewa yarinyar nan mu jika ta da kudi ba? Gashi yanzun Bagauda ya dauke mana ita, kinsan yadda aka yi na tura ki kika nimo mana ita?

   Wallahi baki kyau ta min ba, yarinyar tana da karfin nasara, gashi nan kin janyo mana asarar rasa yan majalisun mu. Gaskiya ki san yadda zakiyi a dawo mana da ita domin itace karfin mu!"

"Kayi hakuri sir zata dawo" ta faɗa tare da barin Office din shi, jikinta yana rawa, yanzun taya zata dawo da Aishafareeda. Yarinyar da tayi bala'in kama kasa.

       Wancan satin da suka tafi Abuja sun ganta da da matar mataimakin shugaban kasa, kuma bata manta da ita ba. Domin ita ta fara mata magana.  Dan haka bata tunanin dan karamin abu zai janyo hankalin yarinyar gare su.

    **
Kasancewar yau Asabar. Tun karfe shida na fito sanye da riga da wando Nikke,na motsa jikina. Sakamakon harin da kiba ya fara kawo min.

   Yan korana wato yan Jagaliyan da aka bani suna take min baya,  suka shiga Bin baya na ina dan gudu a hankali, suma suna take min baya da katafila motar da aka basu irin mara rufin nan. Duk inda na wuce. Abu daya ake fada uwar daba.

     Bayan wuccewarta ya fito yaji ana hiranta.
"Eh wallahi Zakaria ga mace har mace amma yan siyasa sun lalata mata rayuwa. Amma an ce babu abinda Yake hada ta da Bagauda kawai take mishi baya take.

    Amma kuma duk wasu yan iska matan manyan nan suna tare da ita, domin akwai wata yar majalisar dattawa, an ce har gidan Hajiya Turai take zuwa gurin Aishafareeda..
