HAKKIN MALLAKATA
#Sun-Aug-2021
KISHI..!!
~_A Tsakanin Zubda Jini🩸_~

Mai_Dambu

*Sadaukarwa ga Halimatu kangiwa (haliloss). Mrs J Moon. A'isha Fareedah Ya'u Nusaiba Sulaiman Ayuba jikalle na*
              *11*

"Amma dai indai haka ne tab'ata wayonta, na zata mutuniyar kirki ce" inji Zakaria.

"Kai banza kalli yadda ta fito tana gudu, kace mutuniyar kirki? Duba ga yadda kartin maza ke take mata baya."

"Amma Ni dai ina baku shawara ku daina wannan mugun aikin naku, wallahi gulma da yi da mutane duk bai dace ba, taya zaku zauna kuna gulmar mutane? Wannan ba dabi'ar musulmin kwarai bane." Inji Raees.

"Toh a karamakalla, yarinyar nan nayi Imani da Allah, da zaka santa tabbas sai kayi alawadarar abinda take aikatawa, Sarka ce a Kafarta fa, kunnen ta kuwa hujin shi har saman nan ɗinta" Adamu ya nuna bayan kunnen shi.

"Sannan wallahi ka duba hujin hancinta mana, yarinya sai kace karuwa."

"Ai karuwarce ma baki daya kuma tarika a karuwancin domin ba karamar mace bace, duk macen da zata huda hancinta da kunnen ta har zuwa saman kunnen yar kasar sai dai a kira su Lesbian domin tafi kauri tace" mik'ewa Raees yayi ake da musu sallama ya koma cikin gidan,  yana barin gurin Adamu ya kafa gulmar shi.

"Eh mana yana zaune uwar shi ta dauke mishi kome taya zai iya nisa da su, ai idan matar nan ta fadi ya mutu sai yayi kuka da idanun shi." Inji Adamu.

Murmushi Zakaria yayi sannan yace mishi.
"Ba zan iya zagin Raees ba, domin yayi min abinda baka min ba, dan haka ka cire wannan mugun halin naka, wallahi ba zan tab'a gaya mishi ba, amma ka zauna hassada domin mugun abu ne"
"Toh na ce maka ina bakin ciki dashi ne? Daga fadar gaskiya sai ka shiga kirana mai hassada, toh shi kenan"

    "Dai gaya maka ka daina domin kuwa ba abu ne me kyau ba, wallahi baka kyautawa kanka amma sai Allah kadai yasan yadda zai yi da kai" yana fadar haka ya bar shi nan tare da nufar gidan shi.

   Yana barin gurin shima Adamu ya wuce gidan shi.
***
Dakyar da sindin goshi na koma makaranta na yi waec da neco, babu laifi sakamakon yayi kyau aikin, sai dai batun karantun ne naki. Daddy yasa har kasar waje ya fitar dani. Amma fir naki kamar ya tirsassa ni dole nace mishi.
"Wallahi ko ka kai ni ba karatun zanyi ba, gwara ka barni da zamana anan zai fi min!"

Riko hannuna yayi tare da zaunar da ni a saman cinyar shi yana faɗin.
"Dota ina son kiyi karatu, kina da kyau da zaran wani ya aure ki ba zai barki kiyi karatu ba, amma kin ga idan kina tare dani babu wani abu da zai iya faruwa"

     Juyawa nayi tare da kallon shi na mike akan cinyar shi ina faɗin.
"Daddy wannan ba matsala bane, bari na je saloon"
"Nawa ne kudin Saloon din?"
"Dukka babu yawa idan ka bada 30k domin da gyaran jiki za a min."

   "Shi kenan bari na turo miki." Ya fada min, dan dukawa nayi tare da had'a goshina da nashi, sannan na juya na fita, ina d'aga mishi hannu.

    Murmushi yayi wanda shi kad'ai yasan yadda yake ji akan yarinyar, yana mugun son ta. Uwa uba, baya son wani ya rab'e ta. Bai hanata tayi yadda take so ba, sai dai baya son ta faɗa soyayya da wani har ta kai da ya raba shi da ita, yana son ta badan kome ba sai dan rashin tsoronta da iya tafiya da kowa duk da kuwa tana fuskarta wasu abubuwan a cikin ofishin jami'ayyar.

   **
A gajiye ya shiga gidan Umma yana kallon ta, sannan ya zauna tare da sauke ajiyar zuciya.
"Umma Barka da yau!"
"Barka dai Raees ya ka baro su?"
"Da sauki sosai!  Sai dai yana jin jiki fa, domin ciwon ta kama shi sosai."

   "Amma kuma meke damun shi?" Ta tambaya fuskarta yana nuna tausayawa.
"Umma sunce ciwon daji ne, kuma an mishi allura, kuma baya son allura shi yasa baki daya ciwon yayi tsamari dan ko wanda yake kanshi baya ganewa."
"Toh Allah ya bashi lafiya, idan zaka tafi dauko ta sai muje na gaishe su, idan yaso sai na dawo a abin hawa."

    "A'a sai dai na kai ki na dawo dake sai na koma na dauko ta." Ya fada tare da mik'ewa yana hamma.
"Akwai dambun acca a d'akina na zuba maka dan nasan yadda aka kira ta nan ko abinci bata ci ba."
"Wallahi kuwa umma ko karya bata yi ba, shi yasa ma da muke je asibitin sai da na nima."
"Ni ba tambayar ka nayi ba, malam bayani! Ga abinci can nace ka shiga kaci"

   Murmushi yayi sannan ya shige dakin ya zauna yana cin abincin shi. Ko rabi bai kai ba, Adamu ya shigo tare da gaida Umma.
"Umma ina Raees?"
"Yana falo!"
"Toh wallahi yanzun surukinsa Allah yayi mishi rasuwa" 
"Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihi rajioun Ubangiji ya gafarta mishi, Allahu Akbar Allahu Akbar Allahu Akbar, yanzun muke jajjanta halin da bawan Allah nan ya shiga fa"
Da sauri ya fito yana kallon su. Bakin shi cike da dambu,ruwa ya sha tare da fita da sauri, wancan satin da suka je gaida shi abinda ya fada mishi ne ya fara dawo mishi kalma bayan kalma.
_Ahmad nagode sosai da ka rike Halima Ishrat, duk cikin yayunta babu wacce tayi dacen aure kamar ta, babu wacce ta sami mijin da yake kambama alamarinta kamar kai! Duk cikin surukaina kai ne kawai kake kokarin zuwa gaishe ni duk ranar juma'a! Kai mutumin kirki ne! Ba arziki yake saka mutum ya zama na gari ba, abin daga cikin gidan da ka fito ne! Lokacin da na baka auren Halima ban yi zaton Mahaifiyarka zata iya daukar nauyin ku ba! Naga har dauke kai tayi akan ka da Halima duk da nasan bata da kirki! Sai gashi Mahaifiyarka ta zauna da ita kuma ita da kanta take gaya mana halin Mahaifiyarka da irin hakurin! Nasan halin Yarana! Dan haka Ahmad ban dauke ka a matsayin surukina ba! Kai kamar dan da na haifa ne itace nake mata kallon Surukata! Dan haka ka taimaka min ka rike ta don Allah ba dan Ni ba, ba dan na isa ba! Ka taimaka ka fahimci yadda nake ji! Ka rike ta kamar kanwarka wallahi idan ka min haka ka gama min kome! Ahmad duk yayunta babu wacce bata da saki daya saki biyu, wai a haka ma mazajen su, suna cewa Sabida darajata ce suke zaune dasu_

Ya rike hannun Ahmad tare da kallon shi. Alamar yana kara bashi amanar Ishrat ne, cikin natsuwa da wani irin fargaba yace mishi.
*Insha Allah na rike Amanar da ka bani sai dai idan itace ta bukaci rabuwa dani ba zan mata dole ba, amma matukar zata yi hakuri da talauci na,Insha Allah zan rike ta da gaskiya*

  Dan haka ya sallami Ahmad, tare da sawa a kira mishi Ishrat, ya mata nasiha sosai, ya nuna mata rayuwar duniyar ba kome bane, kuma ya bata amanar aurenta, yana faɗin.
"Idan har kina son kwanciyar hankali, ki zauna a dakin ki. Wallahi ina jin a jikina Ahmad Allah zai yalwata mishi al'amarin shi. Don Allah karki sauya daga yadda na sanki, sannan ki cire rowan domin shine matsalar ki."

  Haka yayi mata nasiha sosai, kafin suka bar gidan.

    Abin tausayi haka suka shirya tare da nufar Unguwar su. Halima tare da jiran a kawo gawan. Haka kawai sai yana tuna idan yazo gaishe shi a kofar gidan suke gaisawa shi yake mishi iso har babban falon shi,

   Haka aka kawo gawar, tare da su Ishrat. Tana ganin Ahmad ta tawo gaban shi, tare da fadawa a jikin shi tana kuka. Shi ya shigar da ita har falon Mahaifiyar ta, tana kuka tare da k'amk'ame shi.

"Halima bari muje jana'izar mu dawo kinji!" Ya fada tare da shafa kanta, dakyar yan uwanta suna raba su, bayan an idarda sutura, aka dauke aka mishi sallah sannan aka tafi dashi gidan shi na gaskiya.

  Bayan sun dawo Umma itama ta iso, a nan suka zauna har karfe Biyar, lokacin Nuhu da matar shi suka kawo abincin(kusan ai abokin Raees ne kuma shi yake auren Kanwar Raees ku duba Pagen farko)  can Khadeeja itama ta kawo abincin tuwon shinkafa miyar taushe. Da yaji tantakwashi.

     Ana sallah magarib su umma suka dawo, washi gari kuwa kunun gyada da kosai tayi ta cika katon kularta, aka kai gidan. Da rana aka yi shiga da miya wanda yaji kifi bushashe. Kafin ta bar gida ta daura sanwan na dare, KHADIJA ita zata kawo. 

**
"Gaskiya idan haka ake samun uwar miji, wallahi Halima ki rike mahaifiyar Ahmad, kina gani dai duk cikin yan uwanki babu wacce mijinta ya bada damar a kawo abinci daga gidansa kai ko iyayen su babu wanda yayi tunnain haka. Idan kika zalince ta har ta fusata wallahi zaki sha wahala."

    Haka dangin Ubanta da na Uwarta suke gaya mata, musamman da Umma ta shigo ta musu ta'aziya, sannan ta musu sallama domin zata wani gaisuwar mara lafiya, haba kowa a gurin sai da yayi magana, Nagartan halin Umma, gashi yaranta ma baki dayan su, halinta suka dauko. Har wata Kanwar Maman Ishrat take cewa idan har Salim zai yi aure wallahi zata bashi auren babbar yarta.

      haka aka yi har addu'o'in uku da bakwai, sai da Ishrat tayi sati Uku, kafin ta dawo dan Umma ma cewa tayi ya barta tayi koda kwana Arba'in ne, amma dangin Ubanta suka korota, domin da suka kira Ahmad akan batun cewa yayi.
"A'a ku barta zuwa arba'in, lokacin ta daukewa Hajiya kewa da zaman kad'aici."

"A'a tayi koma d'akinta, ta koma kawai daga can Allah zai amshi addu'arta." Wannan dalilin yasa ta dawo.

    Haka Umma ta dauke musu nauyin girki, domin yawan damuwa da Ishrat ke shiga idan tana girki zaka ji yana konewa sai an shiga a samu tana zaune ta zabga tagumi, wannan yasa Umma ta dauke musu abinci na tsawon wata biyu. Duk wani abinda take yi daga wankin, zuwa wasu yan aikin Ahmad ya dauke mata, haka ta zama very silent.
*
Bayan shekara biyu.

"Dr nace na bar Jam'iyyar GDP, kuma wallahi wani dan iska ya kuma min magana sai naci mutuncin shi. Tsohon kawai, kana samun yadda kake so zaka ce zaka tozarta ni" haka yayi ta musu rashin mutunci, Dr Bagauda bai ce mishi kome ba.

Har ya gama zai fita suka haɗu da Aishafareeda.
"Kina zaune anan ba tare da sanin suwaye kike zaune dasu ba! Gwara ki kama kanki tun wuri..a shirye nake na aure ki"

Dauke kai nayi tare da shiga cikin ofishin.
"Lafiya, Senata Zailani Baidu yake hauka haka?"
"Ya bar Jam'iyyar GDP ne, kuma dama muna da labarin PLP suna niman shi."

     "Akan me? Wani Irin butulci ne haka?"
"Haka suke mishi, taya zai tsaya musu a wayi gari suna juya mishi baya"

"Sabida babu akidar siyasa a ransu baki daya!" Ya fada tare da gyara zaman madubin shi,
"Wata ƙaddara ce ta kawo yarinya karama irinki nan? Koda yake manta! Bagauda toh ya take ne?"

"Lamaran ina cikin damuwa, don Allah ka tafi bana son magana" inji Dr Bagauda,
"Ina zaka so magana tunda ka kunshe ka ajiye, toh iya shi kenan ya fito yayi adawa da antipart da ake yi anan ina kuma sauran sun fito"

"Lamaran don Allah ka fita"
"Kai baka isa ba, sai na gaya maka gaskiya dole ka ajiye abinda kake mallakawa kanka ka fito da akidar siyasa idan ba haka ba, zamu Cigaba da an karar da mutane!"

"Me yasa kake adawa da duk nasarata? Me yasa kake niman bata min suna ne? Toh meye nayi maka da zafi ne? Toh babu damuwa ka gaya musu rashin mutunci ka nuna musu baka da girma sai na jikinka tsohon najadu"
      Inji Dr Bagauda,
     
Tabbas  tunda naga ya fadi haka na Fahimci ranshi yayi mugun b'aci, kuma yana hasale.
"Daddy kayi hakuri!"
"Kar yayi hakuri, amma ka sani Allah yana kallon mu yana sane da abinda muke kokarin b'oyewa."

"Duk abinda nake ina yi ne sabida Al'ummar jahata, shine kake zargina?"

    Dakyar aka fitar da Lamaran Kuli.
    Dakyar muka samu Daddy ya hakura, amma ranshi ya b'aci sosai.

Bamu kuma tsinkewa ba, sai gashi sanatocin sauran yankunan sun canza sheka,  a lokacin idan hankalin Daddy yayi dubu ya tashi domin baki daya har da yan majalisar jahar duk sun sauya sheka. Dan haka Daddy yayi alƙawarin ba zai kuma fitar da wani dan takara ba, sai ya gina shi ya gina mishi siyasa a ranshi yadda kuwa ba zai juyawa kungiyar baya ba...

KISHI..!!
~_A Tsakanin Zubda Jini🩸_~

Mai_Dambu

*Sadaukarwa ga Halimatu kangiwa (haliloss). Mrs J Moon. A'isha Fareedah Ya'u Nusaiba Sulaiman Ayuba jikalle na*
             *12*
_Ina kuke matan ina kuke matan alfarma! Ina matar da ta yarda da kanta toh mu marmatso nesa tazo kusa! A yau wannan kamfanin nan dai ta kuma dawo da karfin ta! Wato oriflame ta dawo muku da kaya masu aminci nagarta uwa Uban kyau ga sauki._
Mayukan gyaran jiki
Turaruka
Kayan kwalliya
Infection
'''Hmm garin garin mu da nisa, amma wannan kina dakinki zai kayanki zai iso gare ki! Ga masu bukatar kayan zasu tuntube wannan Number:+234 808 663 9163 Hajiya Firdausi Malami.... Sai kun zo sayan nagari mai da kudi gida'''
"Dole ka tsayawa kanka tunda tafiyar na kara nuna cin galabar shi, ta kowani fuska dole ka daukaka al'amarin ka, karka sake ka kuma rasa damar mallakar wani shashi zuwa shashi, ka kulle kofar da sunan shi zai habbaka, ga wannan a kai magudanar ruwan garin baki daya a saka."

       "Allah ya gafarta malam har yanzun babu abinda ya kara bayyana na  wancan hasken!" 
"Zai bayyana, ka cire kayanka za ayi maka wanka da jinin dandatsetsiyar budurwa wacce, wacce take tashen balagar ta. Wacce maza suka mata caa. Sai matashiyar budurwan kariyar (wato kare mace) itama zaka sha jininta domin akan haka ne muke niman biyar bukatar ka, ka saka ido alamuranka zasu yi tsammani. Dole ka hakura da sallah La'asar! Asuba! Isha! Sannan idan kazo sujada Bama bukatar yin tasbihi, sunan wannan maradin aljanin zaka kira shi da dangin shi zasu tsaya maka. Dole ka za yi sadaukarwa."

          A hankali ya d'ago idanun shi yana kallon shi malamin, tare da gyada mishi kai. Tare da juya mishi wani yare, sai murmushi yake yana juya kanshi, tare da amincewa. Sannan,

   Haka aka bashi ruwan jinin nan da aka had'a da wasu abubuwan, bawan Allah nan ya shanye kafin aka yi mishi wanka da jinin jaririn da aka yanka a take yana kuka yana kome aka yanke shi, domin ko mahaifa ba a cire mishi ba. Sannan bayan an gama mishi ya shiga wani ruwa fari kyal yayi wanka dashi sannan ya fito, tare da zama aka mishi mishi wani kasko me suffar kokon kan mutum, yayi hayaki dashi. 

"Duk bayan wata uku, za a yanka matashin saurayi da budurwa, sai jariri da barawo. Sai matar da aka daura aurenta.  Sannan wannan ka tabbatar samu yara matasa majiya karfi, masu fama da talauci da wahala, waɗanda kasan kana basu kudi zasu rud'e. Kawai luwadi zaka yi dasu, shine abinda maridin mu yake bukata, ke. Don haka babu ruwanka da wata mace."

  Da sauri ya D'ago kai yana kallon Malam.
"Malam Iyalina fa?"
"Ka gaya musu baka da Lafiya! Idan ba haka ba, na zaka tab'a samun nasara ba. Sai an zubda jini an kuma lullube kome kamar ba ayi ba, karka manta Almajirai da wasu ayi ta sato mana su ana kawo su nan akwai wani Maradin da yake aiki akan duk wani abinda ka tab'a shi jinin Almajirai yake bukata, dan haka a gida gina a bayan gari idan hankalin mutane baya kai ana sato mana su ana kai su can, ga wannan zoben ka kai gidan ka keb'e mishi daki na musamman, abu biyu yaƙe baka a lokaci guda, yana baka kudi da jinin Almajirai yana kuma tafiya da duk wani me kawo maka tsaiko a cikin al'amarin ka. Ina son ka sani babu ruwanka da kowa! Karka Zafaffa. Kuma zamu hana kowa gane alkiblan shi."

Gyada kai yake kawai tare da amincewar dukkanin abinda aka gaya mishi, kuma ya yarda dari bisa dari, sannan ya dauki kayan shi yana faɗin.
"An gama"

   Daga haka ya saka kai ya fita daga cikin Bukkar malam.

***

Sanata Tani Fakare. Waɗannan sune jerin sunayen wadanda ake zargi da cin hanci da rashawa, sannan kujerun mazaban su, da suka ci a karkashin inuwar Jam'iyyar GDP an dawo dasu, sannan za a wakilta wasu amadadin su.. karshen labaran duniyar kenan dani Bukola Haruna Obafemi"

"Allah mun gobe maka, a hankali duk wadanda suka yiwa Bagauda butulci Allah ya tona asirin su, ba. Mamaki saboda kawai dan yana hana su cin hanci ne suka gudu abin su."

" Nima nayi tunanin haka, domin mutum ne mai gaskiya da amana, kuma wallahi ina mamaki yadda yake tsayawa akan ra'ayin shi, gashi dattijon kwarai."

    Yadda Raees ya dage yana sauraron shi su. Kafin ya mike sakawa wani dan acaba bakin mai, yace.
"A tunani na, Bagauda yana da kyakyawan alaka da gwamnatin kasar nan, sannan kuma da ace yana da mugun manufa da tabbas ya bar Jam'iyyar GDP. Duk wanda zai shiga siyasa dole zaka samu sai ya nufi gurin shi. Sannan idan kuka duba baki daya koyi ake da shi. Gaskiya ina matukar daraja Bagauda nan"

       Kallonshi dan acaban yayi tare da cewa.
"Aikuwa muma mun san da haka, domin mutum ne mai alkibla, da sanin ya kama ta. Ba zaka gane haka ba, zai kaje kofar gidan shi da yake G.R.A, kaga yadda mabukata suke zuwa. Kasan Allah yanzun haka sai da na biya kofar gidan na karya. Wallhi babban kula ake fitar mana da abin karyawa. Da shi ake karyawan.

     Wannan mutumin yana bautawa al'ummar shi, kuma ana mugun kaunar shi."

"Baka da mutunci." Zakaria ya fada mishi haka suna dariya, dan haka bayan ya gama juyi mishi mai ya tafi"

"Ya Ahmad Umma ba lafiya da!" Da sauri ya juya tare da shiga cikin gidan, ya samu Khadijah da Ishrat sun gyara mata jiki, babu shiri aka tafi da ita Asibiti.

   Dole abincin Umma ya dawo karkashin kulawar Ahmad, amma haka Matar shi sai ta gadama zata daura abincin, haka Umma taki ta fama, karshe Salim da yayi magana. Suka samu sabani da Ahmad, domin shi sai akan idanun shi ana zuba abincin Umma, kuma da kanta take kawowa.

   Salim ganin halin da mahaifiyar su take ji ciki ya shi tafiya gidan Hajrah Kanwar Raees matar Nuhu, ya  gaya mata. Take kuwa ta shiga zuba musu abincin sau uku a rana.

   Abin sai ya zama abin gulma, Nuhu ya sani sosai, kuma bai tab'a gayawa kowa ba, Adamu da yake munafuki ne, take ya shiga gulmar Raees a duk inda ya zauna, ai gashi nan yaki kula da uwar shi duk wahalar da tasha,  Zakaria ya same shi yana mishi faɗa.
"Adamu wai meye muka tsare maka ne? Kwanaki ka zauna kayi ta gulmar Nuhu, akan matar shi kana taso haɗa su rigima da Aminin shi, bayan kasan cewar matar Nuhu Kanwar Raees ce, haka bai maka ba, ni da Raees muka kashe wannan rigimar.  Yanzun ka tab'o Raees wallhi na kowa tara ne, Raees nashi dozin ne Allah ya baka sa'a munafuki kawai."

"Ina ruwanka, dan koran raees wato dan yana baka na cefane bari ka b'oye halin  uwar shi take ciki. Karya ne nayi? Nan matar nan ta gama hidima da shi, amma dan ya auri yar masu kudi ya juya mata baya, wato sun sami gadon Ubanta shine suke wulaknta uwar shi, ai mata na kawar matar shi ce, wallahi ita ta gaya min Ishrat bata saka musu abinci kuma ta dauke hankalin shi da nuna mishi tana kaunar shi. Bayan bata kaunar mahaifiyar shi, an fada."

Duk abinda yake kara kiba bata ci hatta madara bata sha, gudun kar ya sakata kiba.

     "Nagode sosai, Nuhu"

Abun tausayi ranar a tare suka wuni, babu inda basu shiga ba, babu inda basu zauna ba, suna taya juna kuka.

    _Nasan ko bayan raina Iyalina ba zasu tagayyara ba, domin Raees zai rike min su! Wallahi ina mishi soyayyar amintaka ce ta gaskiya_

Haka kawai kuka yazo mishi ya fashe da kuka , kuka me ban tausayi, dafa shi Bagauda yayi tare da d'ago shi. Suka bar makabarta, yana kuka.

Kai tsaye gidan Umma ya kaishi, bata nutsu ba sai da aka kira likita ya mishi alluran barci, da ruwa ya saka mishi. Ya kwana ya wuni, kafin ya dawo dai-dai. Sannan ya dauke nauyin iyalin Zakaria, ta hanyar daurawa Salim nauyi.

   Sannan ya kuma saka a bincike yan fashin da suka kashe Zakaria, tun da aka yi addu'ar bakwai, Mahaifiyarta Zakaria ta kira Raees ta mika mishi katin bankin. Nan ya sha jinin jikin shi akan wannan katin aka kashe Zakaria! Dan haka ya dauki katin ya dawo da ita, gida.

   Ya samu rashin mutuncin Ishrah, tun daga ranar sai yanayin shi yayi mugun sauya, baya magana inda ba a majalisa ba, baya shiga harkan kowa. Kullum mood din shi kamar wanda aka zage shi. Ita kanta Ishrah bata tsoro yake yi. Domin zai iya kafta mata rashin daraja kafin kuma ya dawo yana bata hakuri.

     Dan haka babu shiri ta nufi hanyar gidan wani boka da yake mata aiki anan wani kauyen zuba, kallon ta yayi sannan yace mata.
"Mutuwar abokin shi yana jijjiga shi, karki matsa dayawa. Kin ja lokacin ki fa, sai dai kadan ya rage mijinki ya dawo daga barcin da kika saka shi, taurarin biyu suna dab da haduwa da juna."

"Yanzun ya zanyi?"  Ta fada muryanta na rawa,
"Yadda zaki yi kenan karki bari ya koma komawa garin matukar ya koma toh akwai aiki a gabanki. Sai dai idan lokacin zaben shi yayi baki isa hana shi ba."

    "Toh fushin fa? Tsoro yake bani"  ta faɗa.
"Zai daina, amma bai zama dole ba, domin abu ne da Umar cikin ranshi da zuciyar shi."

Haka ya bata magani tare da yadda zata yi amfani dashi.

***
Raees yana cikin yan majalisun da talakawa suke so, ga iya turanci ko a gaban shugaban kasa ne yana magana, haka yasa aka shiga nad'a shi a wasu kwamitin ma musamman.

       Ga iya wanka da ɗaukar kayan, idan ya saka kaya kamar wani wanda aka kera kayan a jikin shi ne, sai da ta kai Raees ya amshi lambar yabo sabida ya gwada shi dan kasa ne, duk abinda yasan zai isa gurin talaka bawan Allah nan yana tsaye akan shi, mazabun da suka zabe shi sun fi kowa moran shi.

  Duk yan unguwar su ya taimaka musu ban da Adamu, domin sau biyu yana yunkurin taimaka mishi, yana mafarkin Zakaria cikin zubar jini,  na farko rike hannun shi yayi yana girgiza mishi kai, na biyu kuwa jini na zuba a jikin shi yana kallon shi cikin kuka tare da girgiza mishi kai akan kyautar da zai bawa Adamu, ganin anyi daya anyi biyu, yace ina ba zai yi na uku ba..
Idan Ahmad ya tsaya yana jawabi, sai dai ka ga Igbo da Yoroba sun zuba mishi ido. Sabida yadda yake magana da fidda hakkin kowacce harafi yasa suke masifar tsoron shi, dama ga mutumin Giant ne, a tsaye yake ko irin tumbin nan bayi dashi.

Akwai wani award din da ya samu na sanatan da yafi sauran iya kwalliya, gashi idan Yasaka ko Suit ne, sai hankalin yan mata ya tashi.

      Wannan matsayin da ya samu ya kara habbaka kishinsa da kaunar shi a zuciyar Ishrah. Dan haka take kaf kaf dashi, gani take kamar matan waje zasu cinye mata mijinta, ga wani irin azazzaben dokokin da ta daura mishi.

       Umma ta cigaba da kula damu, matar Salim tayi sanyi sosai, domin kuwa Salim ya kara aure badan kome ba, sai dan yaji ya kamata tana bawa Yar uwanta labarin yadda suka yi zaman kasar waje da Umma, bai sake ta ba, amma ya auri Yar kawuna daga Chad zuwan mu bikin Irfan. Aikuwa ya ganta ya makale mata, tunda ya aurota itama tace sai dai Umma ta dawo gidan su, ni kuwa nace ban zancen ba.

      Haba kamar da wasa muka fara wannan rigimar,ranar na tafi asibiti kai Yarana riga-kafi kawai na dawo na samu sun dauke min Umma da Yarana, murmushi nayi. Haka muka Cigaba da abinda muke da mijina.

    **
Bayan shekara shida domin shekara biyu yai na tenure farko, sannan suka shiga na biyu, kwatsam shugaban kasa ya kwana jinya aka bashi rikon kwarya, karshe da shugaban kasa ya rasu, aka mai dashi shugaban kasa baki daya.

   Duk yadda yaki mulkin dai da Allah ya nufa sai yayi. Bayan sun sauka ne, muka dawo garin mu baki daya da zama, inda duk sati sai mun tafi asibiti ziyara, wata juma'a da muka shiga asibitin na ga abinda ya dame ni, domin ishrah na gani a wani gado, ta kwanjame. Ta koma kamar yar yarinya karama, abin tausayi sai wani yaro me kama da aljani a gefen ta, haka muka ajiye musu kudin ina kuka, murmushi yayi tare da cewa.
"Halimah naso ki, har kwanan gobe ban so A'isha yadda naso ki, amma lokaci guda na samu labarin kina bin Adamu bayan kin san yadda muka yi da shi,, Halima ranar da na dauko ki kin zata nayi barci ne? Kika kira Adamu a kofar gidana kika .... Kika.... Wallahi ban tab'a jin zafin ki ba sai lokacin, domin kin aureni ina Ahmad, Amma daga ke har Adamu Allah ya nuna muku ikon shi hassada da kuka mana ya fado kanku."

 Daga haka ya kalli yaron gwanin ban tausayi.
"My Angel! Ina sonka" kifa hannu nayi a fuskana kamar nice nayi ina kuka. Amma har zuwa lokacin bata saduda ba, domin cewa take idan ta samu lafiya sai ta tozarni.

     Haka rayuwa take, ta kowani fanni ina samun nasara da cigaba, kuma ban tab'a jin zafin mijina ba, Haihuwa na uku yarana biyar. Maza biyu mata uku.
   Sai yaran Zakaria, da yaran Khadeeja, Mai sunan Hajiya Nuriya, me sunan Umma, Ummeeta, sai Mai Abban Hussain.

          Dama Rukayyah tana da Yaranta bakwai. Umar ya sayawa mahaifiyar shi katon gida, a cikin garin ya zuba mata masu kula da gidan, Gwaggonmu itama mun tsaya mata domin gidan Hajiya mun gyara mata an kara gina gidan bayan mun rushe aka yi na zamani, tana cikin flat daya yan haya suna daya.

        ***
Yaran Adamu, guda biyu matan daya da taji sauki bata zauna ba, ta fita yawon domin tunda yan fashi suka mata tabo, ta shiga bariki da gaskenta, karshe wataninta bakwai aka dawo mishi da ita, ciwon zamani ya cimmata.

     Uwar Yaran da take fama da cutar yoyon fitsari ita ke kula da su, haka shi yana ji yana gani ya rasa gefen shi.

   Amma dan girban abinda yayi yana kai kan, domin sauran yarinya daya ce ita kuma tunda abin ya same su, bata cika shiga harkan kowa ba, sai ta zama kamar masu cutar Autism, kamar doluwa, bata son ihu bata son karan abu.

       Allah ka raba mu da cutar hassada.
     ***
Ina rungume da Afnan da take rikici.
"Raees yarinyar nan ta zama rikita'u"
"Ina ga kamar razana ya fa ake"
"Kuma haka ne."

    Sanyin da naji a kaina na duba gefe badi'atul Noor ce ta rike kunnen wani yaron jinsin su.
"Kiyi hakuri! Bata saba ganin shi bane da kaho,, shi yasa take ihu.. amma dan autana ba zai cutar da Kanwar shi ba"

   "Amma wannan yaron sai na zane ka."
Na fada ina niman karamin pillow, take ya koma yadda yake zuwa mata, da sauri ta sauka a cinyata, ta nufi gurin shi, aikuwa ya juya mata baya ta hau bayanshi..yana tashi da ita sama.

    "Nagode sosai Badi'atul Noor, ki gaida min Ummana, yanzun na fahimci yadda rayuwa take, kuma ina kara fahimtar ta yadda ya dace."

"Nice da godiya Kawata abokiyar fada ta, da Fatah kin ajiye aikin ki"

 "Eh na ajiye, kinsan nayiwa Daddy Alkawarin duk lokacin da na samu nasarar kawar da kome zan ajiye aiki toh wancan satin naso na cigaba har na nime rabuwa da Abban su Afnan, yaki amma Alhamdulillahi, na ajiye musu aikin su, na gama nawa"

 "Toh saduwar Alkhairi."

     Ta yi tafiyar ta, bayan ta riko hannun Bilal, suka tafi.

 ...   Nima haka dauki Afnan, zuwa falo na ajiye ta, na wuce dakin shi.
"Daga karshe dai Uwar Daba ta zama na basamude! Kato garjejje"

"Zo nan na gurza miki kayan aikina"

     "Ai sai kayi!"
"Yawwa kinji labarin rasuwar Bagauda! Last week na kai shi Germany, sun ce baki daya aka mishi alluran da zata lalata mishi kome, haka suka mishi.yan dabaru amma ya rasu ban gaya miki bane dan bana son tashin hankalinki, Wali kuma an yanke mishi hukuncin zaman gidan yarin har karshen rayuwar shi, haka ma Alhaji Qarib an. shima haƙa,. Sannan an samu Mulaika Qarib, tana xan wani gidan mahaifinta da ya tsafecceata yana niman Sa'a da ita. Allah yasa muyi kyakyawan karshe itama dai tana nan dai sai yadda hali yaji."

    Ajiyar zuciya na sauke tare da cewa.
"Allah ya gafarta mishi, su kuma Allah ya shirya mu tare da su, yasa abinda ya same su, ya zama Izina ga na bayan mu. Da muna kan mu, Allah ka raba mu da munnan kaddarorrin"

    "Saura Abu daya da baki tab'a fada min ba"

"I love You" na fada ina kallon shi ɗan nasan itace yake jira tsawon shekaru nan. Kuma na fada mishi.

   Alhamdulillahi....
Ban sani ba ko kun fahimci yadda labarin yake, amma Alhamdulillahi ban yake kome ba, ban kuma gajarce muku ba, nagode sosai da yadda suka karanta labarin idan nayi kuskure ku yafe min.... Sannan a koda yaushe ni labarina haka yake. Ban sani ba ko kuna fahimtar kome.

Kunga akwai wata kusan Uku nan gaba so don Allah muyi hakuri, Insha Allah idan ma kammala zan muku magana..
